## What the runner is
`src/pilot_file_drat_v5.py` (SHA-256 b1400f67cc2268bbb572dbe24d3b379b40682fffb0961af460e504a9ced483a2) is a research harness. It runs same-binary controls C1-C7 and then three SAT items, one native child at a time:
- The items are independent-encoder CNFs for a combinatorial-design case split. The encoder is `src/vendor/encode_skel_b.py`, and the expected CNF digests are in `evidence/cnf-pins-v1.json`.
- Solver: kissat 4.0.4, `kissat -q -f --seed=1 --time=600 CNF PROOF_FIFO`. The binary DRAT proof goes into a lane-local FIFO, and an owned sink thread writes it to a proof FILE with a hard cap.
- Checker: `drat-trim CNF PROOF_FILE -i -t 1200` (source in `checker/drat-trim.c`).
- An item may be recorded UNSAT and counted only if `qualify_unsat()` and `terminal_gate()` both pass.

The previous version `src/pilot_file_drat_v3.py` received NO-GO. The exact diff is `src/pilot-runner-v3-to-v5.diff`. Parent findings on v3:
- **P1.** The stability parser rejected the real monitor stream, whose first line is an `observer_start` event; see `control/resource-observer-live-v3-first40.jsonl` and `control/resource_observer_v3.py`. Only the explicit known event schema may be allowed, remaining lines must be strict samples, and the window must be `[time.time()-300, time.time()]`.
- **P2.** v3 could publish UNSAT and count=true after a cancel was latched during or after the checker. A final per-item consumer gate is required before any counted status.
- **P3.** The process status was ambiguous: exit 0 on failures. Explicit nonzero outcomes are required.
- **P4.** The proof cap was only a sampled file size with an assumed overshoot. A hard byte bound, or an honestly scoped soft cap, is required.

## Binding rules the runner must obey (see `control/launch-contract-tightening-v1.json`)
- No SIGKILL, no subprocess timeouts, no ulimit, no deletion or overwrite, no reading of old FIFOs.
- The only allowed signal is a SIGTERM to an owned, identity-safe child, followed by a bounded wait. A survivor must be quarantined (recorded, capacity held), never escalated.
- **Chunk fit:** child end ≤ min(now + 10800 − 120, settled_by − 180), plus 120 s settlement and a 60 s margin.
- **Stability rule** (`control/resource-stability-addendum-v1.json`, monitor path from v2): ≥25 samples spanning ≥290 s in the last 300 s; last sample age ≤30 s; last free ≥ 13.5e9 + projected bytes; max peak-to-later drop ≤ max(3e8, (last_free − 13.5e9)//12); timestamps timezone-aware and strictly increasing; any malformed complete line refuses.
- **Fresh statvfs:** free ≥ 12.2e9 + projected growth, and free − remaining reservations ≥ 12e9.
- **STOP registry:** refuse on any `STOP*`/`HALT*`/`SAT-CANDIDATE*` marker in the lane or run control directories, or on any change to the nine inherited old markers pinned in `evidence/inherited-stop-registry-v1.json`.
- **Streamed proofs:** producer-read and checker-consumed byte counts plus SHA boundaries must be stated exactly.
- **Settled** means: the direct child is reaped, its process group is empty, the streams are closed, the artifacts are hashed, and the receipt is written. Otherwise the run is UNKNOWN or UNSETTLED.

## Review tasks, in priority order
1. **Capped-output overshoot.** Is the proof file truly hard-bounded by `PROOF_CAP`? Are the stdout/stderr capture files truly hard-bounded by `LOG_CAP`? Can any path write beyond them or block a producer forever, for example when a FIFO pipe fills after the cap or the drain stops? Are the reservations (`ITEM_RESERVE`, `CONTROL_RESERVE`) consistent with all retained outputs, including control files?
2. **Post-Popen exception settlement.** Check every exception path after a child or sink exists: Child init, `supervise`, `run_child`, `run_kissat` and `ProofSink.finish`. Can any path orphan a child, leave a FIFO reader thread blocked, signal a reaped or reused PID, signal more than once, or mark something settled when it is not?
3. **Proof-file immutability and consumed count.** Check the FIFO sink semantics, `sink_complete()`, the drat-trim byte count `S->nReads` (`checker/drat-trim.c`, around `read_lit` and `parse`), and the pre/post hashes. Does "read N bytes from proof file" equal the file length for a complete binary proof? Can a truncated or partial proof or CNF still pass? Check the early-EOF behavior (`parse()` early EOF break, formula initialization) against the predicate.
4. **Terminal consumer verdict.** Can any path write `status=UNSAT` or `count_for_scientific_coverage=true` without every predicate and the terminal gate passing, including races with SIGTERM/cancel, registry changes and disk floor? Check the summary and exit-code semantics.
5. **Stability-parser correctness** against the real monitor format and the rule above. Also check the chunk-fit arithmetic and the exit codes.
6. **Controls C1-C7.** Does each control demonstrate its intended reason with the same binaries? Could a control pass vacuously?
7. **Assumptions about kissat behaviour** (the binary is not in the snapshot). Examples: rc 20/10/0, `-q` still printing status lines, `-f` allowing a FIFO proof path, a binary proof by default, SIGTERM handling. Flag every assumption that makes a predicate fail-open; assumptions that only cause fail-closed outcomes are fine.

Return JSON matching the provided schema. Use `severity`=`blocker` only for issues that could produce a wrong UNSAT/count, an orphaned or unsignalled survivor, an unbounded write, or a rule violation.

   101	PROOF_CAP = 1_258_291_200            # HARD: the sink never writes more than this to an item proof file

   103	LOG_CAP = 16 * 1024 * 1024           # HARD per captured stdout+stderr stream
   104	RECORD_MAX = 512 * 1024              # HARD per retained JSON record (bounded() + size check)
   105	STR_MAX = 1000
   106	LIST_MAX = 40

   147	def on_term(signum, frame):
   148	    """r3#2: lock-free; only stamps a scalar order once."""
   149	    if STATE["cancel_order"] is None:
   150	        STATE["cancel_order"] = next(ORDER)

   153	def cancelled():
   154	    return STATE["cancel_order"] is not None

   157	def latch(reason):
   158	    LATCH.append(str(reason)[:STR_MAX])

   161	def clock():
   162	    return subprocess.run(["/bin/date", "+%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S%z"], capture_output=True, text=True,
   163	                          check=True, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL).stdout.strip()

   165	
   166	def bounded(x):
   167	    """r3#7: bound strings/lists recursively, replacing removed text by its SHA-256."""
   168	    if isinstance(x, str):
   169	        if len(x) <= STR_MAX:
   170	            return x
   171	        return x[:STR_MAX] + "...[truncated %d chars sha256 %s]" % (len(x), hashlib.sha256(x.encode()).hexdigest())
   172	    if isinstance(x, (list, tuple)):
   173	        y = [bounded(v) for v in x[:LIST_MAX]]
   174	        if len(x) > LIST_MAX:
   175	            y.append("...[%d more items]" % (len(x) - LIST_MAX))
   176	        return y
   177	    if isinstance(x, dict):
   178	        return {str(k)[:200]: bounded(v) for k, v in x.items()}
   179	    return x
   180	
   181	
   182	def serialize(obj):
   183	    data = json.dumps(bounded(obj), indent=1, sort_keys=True).encode()
   184	    if len(data) > RECORD_MAX:
   185	        raise RuntimeError("record exceeds RECORD_MAX (%d bytes)" % len(data))
   186	    return data
   187	
   188	
   189	def write_x(path, obj):
   190	    data = serialize(obj)
   191	    with open(path, "xb") as f:
   192	        f.write(data)
   193	        f.flush()
   194	        os.fsync(f.fileno())
   195	    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
   196	
   197	
   198	def write_bytes_x(path, data):
   199	    with open(path, "xb") as f:
   200	        f.write(data)
   201	        f.flush()
   202	        os.fsync(f.fileno())
   203	    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest(), len(data)
   204	
   205	
   206	def sha_file(path):
   207	    st = os.lstat(path)
   208	    if not stat.S_ISREG(st.st_mode):
   209	        raise RuntimeError("not a regular file: " + path)
   210	    h = hashlib.sha256()
   211	    n = 0
   212	    with open(path, "rb") as f:
   213	        for b in iter(lambda: f.read(1 << 20), b""):
   214	            h.update(b)
   215	            n += len(b)
   216	    return h.hexdigest(), n
   217	
   218	
   219	def read_pinned(path, expect):
   220	    st = os.lstat(path)
   221	    if not stat.S_ISREG(st.st_mode):
   222	        raise RuntimeError("not a regular file: " + path)
   223	    with open(path, "rb") as f:
   224	        raw = f.read()
   225	    got = hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
   226	    if got != expect:
   227	        raise RuntimeError(f"pin mismatch {path}: {got} != {expect}")
   228	    return raw
   229	
   230	
   231	def free_bytes():
   232	    st = os.statvfs(LANE)
   233	    return st.f_bavail * st.f_frsize
   234	
   235	
   236	def ps_table():
   237	    """Process table rows (pid, ppid, pgid, rss_kib); raises on failure (callers latch)."""
   238	    out = subprocess.run(["/bin/ps", "-A", "-o", "pid=,ppid=,pgid=,rss="], capture_output=True, text=True,
   239	                         stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL)
   240	    if out.returncode != 0:
   241	        raise RuntimeError("ps -A failed rc %d" % out.returncode)
   242	    rows = []
   243	    for ln in out.stdout.splitlines():
   244	        parts = ln.split()
   245	        if len(parts) != 4 or not all(p.isdigit() for p in parts):
   246	            raise RuntimeError("malformed ps row %r" % ln)
   247	        rows.append(tuple(int(p) for p in parts))
   248	    if not rows:
   249	        raise RuntimeError("empty ps table")
   250	    return rows
   251	
   252	
   253	def identity_checked(pid, runner_pid):
   254	    """Start identity; must parse and show ppid==runner and pgid==pid (own session)."""
   255	    out = subprocess.run(["/bin/ps", "-o", "pid=,ppid=,pgid=,lstart=,comm=", "-p", str(pid)],
   256	                         capture_output=True, text=True, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL)
   257	    line = out.stdout.strip()
   258	    parts = line.split()
   259	    if out.returncode != 0 or len(parts) < 8 or not all(p.isdigit() for p in parts[:3]):
   260	        raise RuntimeError("unavailable/malformed start identity %r" % line)

 succeeded in 0ms:
   261	    if int(parts[0]) != pid or int(parts[1]) != runner_pid or int(parts[2]) != pid:
   262	        raise RuntimeError("start identity mismatch %r" % line)
   263	    return line

   265	
   266	def lane_files():
   267	    """r3#4/#5: (path -> (inode, size)) for every non-directory entry under the run output tree and
   268	    LANE/tmp (the trees a misbehaving child could reach through its paths/cwd; symlinks not followed)."""
   269	    snap = {}
   270	    for root in (STATE["out"], LANE + "/tmp"):
   271	        for dp, dns, fns in os.walk(root, followlinks=False):
   272	            for n in fns:
   273	                p = os.path.join(dp, n)
   274	                st = os.lstat(p)
   275	                snap[p] = (st.st_ino, st.st_size)
   276	    return snap
   277	
   278	
   279	def registry_refusals():
   280	    """Fail-closed STOP registry: new-round STOP*/HALT*/SAT-CANDIDATE* markers in LANE/control or
   281	    RUN/control; any change, loss or addition in the inherited old cycle2-root STOP/HALT set."""
   282	    why = []
   283	    for d in (LANE + "/control", RUN + "/control"):
   284	        for name in sorted(os.listdir(d)):
   285	            if re.match(r"(?i)^(stop|halt|sat-candidate)", name) or "HALT" in name:
   286	                why.append("new-round marker " + os.path.join(d, name))
   287	    c2 = OLD + "/cycle2"
   288	    present = sorted(n for n in os.listdir(c2) if re.match(r"^(STOP|HALT)", n) or "HALT" in n)
   289	    if present != sorted(OLD_LIVE_MARKERS):
   290	        why.append("inherited cycle2 marker set changed: " + json.dumps(present))
   291	    for name, h in OLD_LIVE_MARKERS.items():
   292	        try:
   293	            if sha_file(os.path.join(c2, name))[0] != h:
   294	                why.append("inherited marker changed: " + name)
   295	        except Exception as exc:
   296	            why.append(f"inherited marker unreadable: {name}: {exc!r}")
   297	    try:
   298	        if sha_file(REGISTRY[0])[0] != REGISTRY[1]:
   299	            why.append("lane registry changed")
   300	        if sha_file(LAUNCH_CONTRACT[0])[0] != LAUNCH_CONTRACT[1]:
   301	            why.append("launch contract changed")
   302	    except Exception as exc:
   303	        why.append("registry/contract unreadable: %r" % exc)
   304	    return why
   305	
   306	
   307	def _aware(s):
   308	    dt = datetime.fromisoformat(s)
   309	    if dt.tzinfo is None:
   310	        raise ValueError("naive timestamp")
   311	    return dt
   312	
   313	
   314	def _int(x, lo=None):
   315	    if not isinstance(x, int) or isinstance(x, bool) or (lo is not None and x < lo):
   316	        raise ValueError("bad integer %r" % (x,))
   317	    return x
   318	
   319	
   320	def stability_refusals(projected):
   321	    """resource-stability-addendum v1 rule over the v2-pinned v3 monitor; strict schema."""
   322	    st = os.lstat(MONITOR)
   323	    if not stat.S_ISREG(st.st_mode):
   324	        return ["monitor is not a regular file"]
   325	    with open(MONITOR, "rb") as f:
   326	        raw = f.read()
   327	    complete = raw.split(b"\n")[:-1]  # last element: b"" after a final newline, or an ignored partial line
   328	    rows = []
   329	    for i, ln in enumerate(complete):
   330	        try:
   331	            o = json.loads(ln)
   332	            if not isinstance(o, dict):
   333	                raise ValueError("not an object")
   334	            if "event" in o:
   335	                if not (i == 0 and set(o) == {"event", "at", "pid", "pgid", "cwd", "source_sha256"}
   336	                        and o["event"] == "observer_start" and o["source_sha256"] == OBSERVER_SOURCE_SHA
   337	                        and o["cwd"] == OBSERVER_CWD):
   338	                    return ["monitor event line %d is not the allowed first observer_start: %r" % (i, ln[:160])]
   339	                _aware(o["at"])
   340	                _int(o["pid"], 1)
   341	                _int(o["pgid"], 1)
   342	                continue
   343	            if set(o) != {"t", "free", "floor_bytes", "scope"} or o["floor_bytes"] != 12_000_000_000 \
   344	                    or o["scope"] != SAMPLE_SCOPE:
   345	                raise ValueError("sample schema/fixed values")
   346	            _int(o["floor_bytes"], 0)
   347	            rows.append((_aware(o["t"]).timestamp(), _int(o["free"], 0)))
   348	        except Exception as exc:
   349	            return ["malformed monitor line %d: %r (%r)" % (i, ln[:160], exc)]
   350	    if not rows:
   351	        return ["no monitor samples"]
   352	    for a, b in zip(rows, rows[1:]):
   353	        if not b[0] > a[0]:
   354	            return ["monitor timestamps not strictly increasing"]
   355	    why = []
   356	    now = time.time()
   357	    t_last, free_last = rows[-1]
   358	    if not (0 <= now - t_last <= 30):
   359	        why.append(f"monitor last sample age {now - t_last:.1f}s outside [0,30]")
   360	    win = [r for r in rows if now - 300 <= r[0] <= now]
   361	    if len(win) < 25:
   362	        why.append(f"only {len(win)} samples in [now-300, now]")
   363	    elif win[-1][0] - win[0][0] < 290:
   364	        why.append(f"window span {win[-1][0] - win[0][0]:.1f}s < 290 s")
   365	    if free_last < 13_500_000_000 + projected:
   366	        why.append(f"last free {free_last} < 13.5e9 + projected {projected}")
   367	    allowed = max(300_000_000, (free_last - 13_500_000_000) // 12)
   368	    peak, drop = None, 0
   369	    for _, fr in win:
   370	        peak = fr if peak is None else max(peak, fr)
   371	        drop = max(drop, peak - fr)
   372	    if drop > allowed:
   373	        why.append(f"peak-to-later drop {drop} > allowed {allowed}")
   374	    return why
   375	
   376	
   377	def full_admission(projected, child_deadline):
   378	    """Blocking part of admission (registry, monitor, statvfs, fit). Returns refusal reasons."""
   379	    why = []
   380	    try:
   381	        why += registry_refusals()
   382	        why += stability_refusals(projected)
   383	        fb = free_bytes()
   384	        if fb < FRESH_BASE + projected:
   385	            why.append(f"fresh free {fb} < 12.2e9 + {projected}")
   386	        if fb - STATE["remaining_reserve"] < FREE_FLOOR:
   387	            why.append(f"free {fb} - remaining reservations {STATE['remaining_reserve']} < 12e9")
   388	    except Exception as exc:
   389	        why.append("admission exception %r" % exc)
   390	    if child_deadline + SETTLE_TAIL > STATE["abs_end"] or child_deadline + SETTLE_TAIL + MARGIN > STATE["chunk_end"] \
   391	            or child_deadline + SETTLE_TAIL > OWNED_SETTLED_BY - 180:
   392	        why.append("per-child chunk fit / absolute end (with full settlement tail)")
   393	    return why
   394	
   395	
   396	class ProofSink:
   397	    """Owned FIFO reader writing the proof FILE with a HARD byte cap."""
   398	
   399	    def __init__(self, fifo_path, file_path, cap):
   400	        if os.path.lexists(fifo_path) or os.path.lexists(file_path):
   401	            raise RuntimeError("proof fifo/file path exists")
   402	        os.mkfifo(fifo_path, 0o600)
   403	        self.fifo_ino = os.lstat(fifo_path).st_ino
   404	        self.fifo_path, self.file_path, self.cap = fifo_path, file_path, cap
   405	        self.file = open(file_path, "xb")
   406	        self.sha = hashlib.sha256()
   407	        self.written = self.received = self.discarded = 0
   408	        self.over_cap = self.connected = self.eof = False
   409	        self.error = None
   410	        self.unblocked_by_runner = False
   411	        self.finished = None
   412	        self.thread = threading.Thread(target=self._run, daemon=True)
   413	        self.thread.start()
   414	
   415	    def _run(self):
   416	        try:
   417	            fd = os.open(self.fifo_path, os.O_RDONLY)  # blocks until a writer opens the FIFO
   418	            self.connected = True
   419	            try:
   420	                while True:
   421	                    b = os.read(fd, 1 << 20)
   422	                    if not b:
   423	                        self.eof = True
   424	                        break
   425	                    self.received += len(b)
   426	                    if not self.over_cap and self.written + len(b) <= self.cap:
   427	                        self.file.write(b)
   428	                        self.sha.update(b)
   429	                        self.written += len(b)
   430	                    else:
   431	                        self.over_cap = True       # never write beyond the cap; drain so the producer can exit
   432	                        self.discarded += len(b)
   433	            finally:
   434	                os.close(fd)
   435	        except Exception as exc:
   436	            self.error = repr(exc)
   437	        finally:
   438	            try:
   439	                self.file.flush()
   440	                os.fsync(self.file.fileno())
   441	                self.file.close()
   442	            except Exception as exc:
   443	                self.error = (self.error or "") + " close:" + repr(exc)
   444	
   445	    def finish(self):
   446	        """After the producer is reaped: unblock a never-connected reader, bounded join; latches on an
   447	        unsettled thread, sink I/O error, re-hash failure or a replaced FIFO."""
   448	        if self.finished is not None:
   449	            return self.finished
   450	        deadline = time.time() + SINK_UNBLOCK
   451	        while self.thread.is_alive() and not self.connected and time.time() < deadline:
   452	            try:
   453	                fd = os.open(self.fifo_path, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_NONBLOCK)
   454	                self.unblocked_by_runner = True
   455	                os.close(fd)
   456	                break
   457	            except OSError as exc:
   458	                if exc.errno != errno.ENXIO:
   459	                    self.error = (self.error or "") + " unblock:" + repr(exc)
   460	                    break
   461	                time.sleep(0.05)
   462	        self.thread.join(STREAM_JOIN)
   463	        alive = self.thread.is_alive()
   464	        rec = {"fifo": self.fifo_path, "file": self.file_path, "cap": self.cap, "connected": self.connected,
   465	               "eof": self.eof, "received": self.received, "written": self.written, "discarded": self.discarded,
   466	               "over_cap": self.over_cap, "error": self.error, "unblocked_by_runner": self.unblocked_by_runner,
   467	               "thread_alive_after_join": alive, "sha256_of_written": self.sha.hexdigest()}
   468	        try:
   469	            st = os.lstat(self.fifo_path)
   470	            rec["fifo_same_inode_still_fifo"] = bool(stat.S_ISFIFO(st.st_mode) and st.st_ino == self.fifo_ino)
   471	        except Exception as exc:
   472	            rec["fifo_same_inode_still_fifo"] = False
   473	            rec["fifo_check_error"] = repr(exc)
   474	        if not rec["fifo_same_inode_still_fifo"]:
   475	            latch("proof FIFO replaced or missing: " + self.fifo_path)
   476	        if alive:
   477	            latch("proof sink thread unsettled: " + self.fifo_path)
   478	        else:
   479	            try:
   480	                rec["file_rehash"] = list(sha_file(self.file_path))
   481	            except Exception as exc:
   482	                rec["file_rehash_error"] = repr(exc)
   483	                latch("proof file re-hash failed %r" % exc)
   484	        if self.error:
   485	            latch("proof sink I/O error %s" % self.error)
   486	        rec["sink_settled"] = (not alive and self.error is None and "file_rehash" in rec
   487	                               and rec["fifo_same_inode_still_fifo"])
   488	        self.finished = rec
   489	        return rec
   490	
   491	
   492	class Child:
   493	    """One owned native child: pre-allocated ownership, own session, HARD capped capture, records."""
   494	
   495	    def __init__(self, argv, label, wall, reserve, inputs, sink, cwd):
   496	        STATE["seq"] += 1
   497	        self.seq = STATE["seq"]
   498	        self.argv, self.label, self.sink_obj, self.cwd = argv, label, sink, cwd
   499	        base = os.path.join(STATE["out"], "children", "%02d-%s" % (self.seq, label))
   500	        self.out_path, self.launch_path, self.settle_path = base + ".out", base + ".launch.json", base + ".settle.json"
   501	        self.quarantine_path = base + ".QUARANTINE.json"
   502	        self.captured, self.over_cap, self.reader_error = 0, False, None
   503	        self.term_sent_at, self.stop, self.reader, self.settled_rec = None, None, None, None
   504	        self.identity_at_start, self.descendants = None, []
   505	        self.inputs, self.reserve = inputs, reserve
   506	        self.proc, self.pid, self.pgid, self.started = None, None, None, None
   507	        self.quarantined, self.refused = False, None
   508	        self.lane_before = None
   509	        self.capture = open(self.out_path, "xb")  # outputs prepared BEFORE admission and spawn
   510	        now = time.time()
   511	        self.deadline = min(now + wall + GRACE, STATE["abs_end"] - SETTLE_TAIL)
   512	        why = full_admission(reserve, self.deadline)
   513	        if LATCH:
   514	            why.append("latched: %r" % LATCH)
   515	        if cancelled():
   516	            why.append("owner cancel")
   517	        if time.time() + wall > self.deadline:
   518	            why.append("frozen child deadline already too close")
   519	        if why:
   520	            self.capture.close()

   320	def stability_refusals(projected):
   321	    """resource-stability-addendum v1 rule over the v2-pinned v3 monitor; strict schema."""
   322	    st = os.lstat(MONITOR)
   323	    if not stat.S_ISREG(st.st_mode):
   324	        return ["monitor is not a regular file"]
   325	    with open(MONITOR, "rb") as f:
   326	        raw = f.read()
   327	    complete = raw.split(b"\n")[:-1]  # last element: b"" after a final newline, or an ignored partial line
   328	    rows = []
   329	    for i, ln in enumerate(complete):
   330	        try:
   331	            o = json.loads(ln)
   332	            if not isinstance(o, dict):
   333	                raise ValueError("not an object")
   334	            if "event" in o:
   335	                if not (i == 0 and set(o) == {"event", "at", "pid", "pgid", "cwd", "source_sha256"}
   336	                        and o["event"] == "observer_start" and o["source_sha256"] == OBSERVER_SOURCE_SHA
   337	                        and o["cwd"] == OBSERVER_CWD):
   338	                    return ["monitor event line %d is not the allowed first observer_start: %r" % (i, ln[:160])]
   339	                _aware(o["at"])
   340	                _int(o["pid"], 1)
   341	                _int(o["pgid"], 1)
   342	                continue
   343	            if set(o) != {"t", "free", "floor_bytes", "scope"} or o["floor_bytes"] != 12_000_000_000 \
   344	                    or o["scope"] != SAMPLE_SCOPE:
   345	                raise ValueError("sample schema/fixed values")
   346	            _int(o["floor_bytes"], 0)
   347	            rows.append((_aware(o["t"]).timestamp(), _int(o["free"], 0)))
   348	        except Exception as exc:
   349	            return ["malformed monitor line %d: %r (%r)" % (i, ln[:160], exc)]
   350	    if not rows:
   351	        return ["no monitor samples"]
   352	    for a, b in zip(rows, rows[1:]):
   353	        if not b[0] > a[0]:
   354	            return ["monitor timestamps not strictly increasing"]
   355	    why = []
   356	    now = time.time()
   357	    t_last, free_last = rows[-1]
   358	    if not (0 <= now - t_last <= 30):
   359	        why.append(f"monitor last sample age {now - t_last:.1f}s outside [0,30]")
   360	    win = [r for r in rows if now - 300 <= r[0] <= now]
   361	    if len(win) < 25:
   362	        why.append(f"only {len(win)} samples in [now-300, now]")
   363	    elif win[-1][0] - win[0][0] < 290:
   364	        why.append(f"window span {win[-1][0] - win[0][0]:.1f}s < 290 s")
   365	    if free_last < 13_500_000_000 + projected:
   366	        why.append(f"last free {free_last} < 13.5e9 + projected {projected}")
   367	    allowed = max(300_000_000, (free_last - 13_500_000_000) // 12)
   368	    peak, drop = None, 0
   369	    for _, fr in win:
   370	        peak = fr if peak is None else max(peak, fr)
   371	        drop = max(drop, peak - fr)
   372	    if drop > allowed:
   373	        why.append(f"peak-to-later drop {drop} > allowed {allowed}")
   374	    return why

   377	def full_admission(projected, child_deadline):
   378	    """Blocking part of admission (registry, monitor, statvfs, fit). Returns refusal reasons."""
   379	    why = []
   380	    try:
   381	        why += registry_refusals()
   382	        why += stability_refusals(projected)
   383	        fb = free_bytes()
   384	        if fb < FRESH_BASE + projected:
   385	            why.append(f"fresh free {fb} < 12.2e9 + {projected}")
   386	        if fb - STATE["remaining_reserve"] < FREE_FLOOR:
   387	            why.append(f"free {fb} - remaining reservations {STATE['remaining_reserve']} < 12e9")
   388	    except Exception as exc:
   389	        why.append("admission exception %r" % exc)
   390	    if child_deadline + SETTLE_TAIL > STATE["abs_end"] or child_deadline + SETTLE_TAIL + MARGIN > STATE["chunk_end"] \
   391	            or child_deadline + SETTLE_TAIL > OWNED_SETTLED_BY - 180:
   392	        why.append("per-child chunk fit / absolute end (with full settlement tail)")
   393	    return why

   396	class ProofSink:
   397	    """Owned FIFO reader writing the proof FILE with a HARD byte cap."""
   398	
   399	    def __init__(self, fifo_path, file_path, cap):
   400	        if os.path.lexists(fifo_path) or os.path.lexists(file_path):
   401	            raise RuntimeError("proof fifo/file path exists")
   402	        os.mkfifo(fifo_path, 0o600)
   403	        self.fifo_ino = os.lstat(fifo_path).st_ino
   404	        self.fifo_path, self.file_path, self.cap = fifo_path, file_path, cap
   405	        self.file = open(file_path, "xb")
   406	        self.sha = hashlib.sha256()
   407	        self.written = self.received = self.discarded = 0
   408	        self.over_cap = self.connected = self.eof = False
   409	        self.error = None
   410	        self.unblocked_by_runner = False
   411	        self.finished = None
   412	        self.thread = threading.Thread(target=self._run, daemon=True)
   413	        self.thread.start()
   414	
   415	    def _run(self):
   416	        try:
   417	            fd = os.open(self.fifo_path, os.O_RDONLY)  # blocks until a writer opens the FIFO
   418	            self.connected = True
   419	            try:
   420	                while True:
   421	                    b = os.read(fd, 1 << 20)
   422	                    if not b:
   423	                        self.eof = True
   424	                        break
   425	                    self.received += len(b)
   426	                    if not self.over_cap and self.written + len(b) <= self.cap:
   427	                        self.file.write(b)
   428	                        self.sha.update(b)
   429	                        self.written += len(b)
   430	                    else:
   431	                        self.over_cap = True       # never write beyond the cap; drain so the producer can exit
   432	                        self.discarded += len(b)
   433	            finally:
   434	                os.close(fd)
   435	        except Exception as exc:
   436	            self.error = repr(exc)
   437	        finally:
   438	            try:
   439	                self.file.flush()
   440	                os.fsync(self.file.fileno())
   441	                self.file.close()
   442	            except Exception as exc:
   443	                self.error = (self.error or "") + " close:" + repr(exc)
   444	
   445	    def finish(self):

   504	        self.identity_at_start, self.descendants = None, []
   505	        self.inputs, self.reserve = inputs, reserve
   506	        self.proc, self.pid, self.pgid, self.started = None, None, None, None
   507	        self.quarantined, self.refused = False, None
   508	        self.lane_before = None
   509	        self.capture = open(self.out_path, "xb")  # outputs prepared BEFORE admission and spawn
   510	        now = time.time()
   511	        self.deadline = min(now + wall + GRACE, STATE["abs_end"] - SETTLE_TAIL)
   512	        why = full_admission(reserve, self.deadline)
   513	        if LATCH:
   514	            why.append("latched: %r" % LATCH)
   515	        if cancelled():
   516	            why.append("owner cancel")
   517	        if time.time() + wall > self.deadline:
   518	            why.append("frozen child deadline already too close")
   519	        if why:
   520	            self.capture.close()
   521	            self.refused = why
   522	            return
   523	        if sink is not None:
   524	            self.lane_before = lane_files()
   525	        OWNED.append(self)  # r3#1: ownership exists before the process does
   526	        self.proc = subprocess.Popen(argv, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
   527	                                     start_new_session=True, close_fds=True, cwd=cwd, env=CHILD_ENV)
   528	        try:
   529	            self.pid = self.pgid = self.proc.pid
   530	            self.started = time.time()
   531	            self.identity_at_start = identity_checked(self.pid, os.getpid())
   532	            write_x(self.launch_path, {"seq": self.seq, "label": label, "runner_pid": os.getpid(), "pid": self.pid,
   533	                                       "pgid": self.pgid, "identity": self.identity_at_start, "argv": argv,
   534	                                       "cwd": cwd, "start_epoch": self.started,
   535	                                       "absolute_deadline_epoch": self.deadline, "capture": self.out_path,
   536	                                       "capture_cap": LOG_CAP, "retained_reservation_bytes": reserve,
   537	                                       "inputs": inputs, "run_launch_record": STATE["run_record"],
   538	                                       "go": STATE["go"], "runner_sha256": STATE["runner_sha"],
   539	                                       "binaries": {"kissat": KISSAT, "drat_trim": [DRAT, DRAT_OLD[1]]}})
   540	            print("CHILD_LAUNCH", self.launch_path, flush=True)
   541	            self.reader = threading.Thread(target=self._read, daemon=True)
   542	            self.reader.start()
   543	        except BaseException as exc:
   544	            latch("post-Popen registration failure %r" % (exc,))
   545	            if self.stop is None:
   546	                self.stop = "registration_failure"
   547	            self.reap_robust()
   548	            raise
   549	
   550	    def _read(self):
   551	        try:
   552	            while True:
   553	                b = self.proc.stdout.read1(65536)
   554	                if not b:
   555	                    break
   556	                room = LOG_CAP - self.captured
   557	                if room > 0:
   558	                    self.capture.write(b[:room])
   559	                self.captured += len(b)
   560	                if self.captured > LOG_CAP:
   561	                    self.over_cap = True
   562	        except Exception as exc:
   563	            self.reader_error = repr(exc)
   564	        finally:
   565	            try:
   566	                self.capture.flush()
   567	                os.fsync(self.capture.fileno())
   568	                self.capture.close()
   569	                self.proc.stdout.close()
   570	            except Exception as exc:
   571	                self.reader_error = (self.reader_error or "") + " close:" + repr(exc)
   572	
   573	    def alive(self):
   574	        return self.proc.poll() is None
   575	
   576	    def term(self, reason):
   577	        if self.term_sent_at is None and self.proc.returncode is None:
   578	            self.stop = reason
   579	            self.term_sent_at = time.time()
   580	            self.proc.send_signal(signal.SIGTERM)  # Popen polls first; never signals a reaped PID
   581	
   582	    def reap_robust(self):
   583	        """r3#1: exception-resistant reap state machine; retries on any exception, never unwinds
   584	        before wait() returns.  Single SIGTERM (only if requested), quarantine at the 60 s transition."""
   585	        while True:
   586	            try:
   587	                if self.term_sent_at is None and self.stop is not None:
   588	                    self.term_sent_at = time.time()
   589	                    self.proc.send_signal(signal.SIGTERM)
   590	                if self.term_sent_at is not None and not self.quarantined:
   591	                    while self.proc.poll() is None and time.time() < self.term_sent_at + TERM_WAIT:
   592	                        time.sleep(0.1)
   593	                    if self.proc.poll() is None:
   594	                        self.quarantined = True
   595	                        latch("quarantine pid %d" % self.pid)
   596	                        try:
   597	                            write_x(self.quarantine_path, {"pid": self.pid, "pgid": self.pgid, "argv": self.argv,
   598	                                                           "epoch": time.time(), "note": "survived SIGTERM + 60 s; "
   599	                                                           "no further signal; runner keeps waiting"})
   600	                        except BaseException:
   601	                            pass
   602	                return self.proc.wait()
   603	            except BaseException:
   604	                time.sleep(0.05)
   605	                continue
   606	
   607	    def census(self):
   608	        """Other processes with ppid==child or pgid==child (excluding the child itself)."""
   609	        rows = ps_table()
   610	        found = [r for r in rows if r[0] != self.pid and (r[1] == self.pid or r[2] == self.pgid)]
   611	        rss = sum(r[3] for r in rows if r[0] == self.pid or r in found) * 1024
   612	        return found, rss
   613	
   614	    def settle(self):
   615	        """Always reaches wait(); settlement final only after streams, sink, FIFO/cwd/lane checks,
   616	        hashes and the receipt; any failure clears 'settled' and latches."""
   617	        if self.settled_rec is not None:
   618	            return self.settled_rec
   619	        problems = []
   620	        rc = self.reap_robust()
   621	        stream_alive = None
   622	        if self.reader is not None:
   623	            self.reader.join(STREAM_JOIN)
   624	            stream_alive = self.reader.is_alive()
   625	        else:
   626	            problems.append("no capture reader")
   627	            try:
   628	                self.capture.close()
   629	            except Exception as exc:
   630	                problems.append("capture close %r" % exc)
   631	        group_empty = None
   632	        try:
   633	            os.killpg(self.pgid, 0)
   634	            group_empty = False
   635	        except ProcessLookupError:
   636	            group_empty = True
   637	        except BaseException as exc:
   638	            problems.append("killpg probe %r" % exc)
   639	        try:
   640	            after, _ = self.census()
   641	            if after:
   642	                problems.append("descendants after reap %r" % after)
   643	        except BaseException as exc:
   644	            problems.append("post-reap census failed %r" % exc)
   645	        if self.descendants:
   646	            problems.append("descendants seen during run %r" % self.descendants[:5])
   647	        sink_rec = None
   648	        if self.sink_obj is not None:
   649	            try:
   650	                sink_rec = self.sink_obj.finish()
   651	                if not sink_rec["sink_settled"]:
   652	                    problems.append("proof sink not settled")
   653	            except BaseException as exc:
   654	                problems.append("sink finish failed %r" % exc)
   655	        try:
   656	            if os.listdir(self.cwd):
   657	                problems.append("child cwd not empty: %r" % os.listdir(self.cwd)[:10])
   658	        except BaseException as exc:
   659	            problems.append("cwd check failed %r" % exc)
   660	        if self.lane_before is not None:
   661	            try:
   662	                expected = {self.out_path, self.launch_path, self.settle_path, self.quarantine_path}
   663	                if self.sink_obj is not None:
   664	                    expected.add(self.sink_obj.file_path)
   665	                now = lane_files()
   666	                unexpected = [p for p, v in now.items()
   667	                              if p not in expected and (p not in self.lane_before or self.lane_before[p] != v)]
   668	                if unexpected:
   669	                    problems.append("unexpected lane file changes %r" % unexpected[:10])
   670	            except BaseException as exc:
   671	                problems.append("lane census failed %r" % exc)
   672	        diag = []
   673	        out_sha = None
   674	        if stream_alive is False:
   675	            try:
   676	                out_sha = sha_file(self.out_path)[0]
   677	                with open(self.out_path, "rb") as f:
   678	                    diag = sorted(set(m.group(0) for m in RESOURCE_DIAG.finditer(f.read().decode("utf-8", "replace"))))
   679	            except BaseException as exc:
   680	                problems.append("capture hash/scan failed %r" % exc)
   681	        if diag:
   682	            latch("native memory diagnostic in %s: %r" % (self.label, diag))  # r3#3
   683	        rec = {"seq": self.seq, "label": self.label, "pid": self.pid, "pgid": self.pgid,
   684	               "identity_at_start": self.identity_at_start, "argv": self.argv, "returncode": rc,
   685	               "stop": self.stop or "self", "seconds": round(time.time() - self.started, 3),
   686	               "captured_bytes": self.captured, "capture_over_cap": self.over_cap, "reader_error": self.reader_error,
   687	               "stream_thread_alive_after_join": stream_alive, "group_empty_after_reap": group_empty,
   688	               "quarantined": self.quarantined, "problems": problems, "resource_diagnostics": diag,
   689	               "proof_sink": sink_rec, "launch_record": self.launch_path, "output_path": self.out_path,
   690	               "output_sha256": out_sha, "settled": False}
   691	        ok = (stream_alive is False and group_empty is True and not self.quarantined and not problems
   692	              and self.reader_error is None and out_sha is not None)
   693	        try:
   694	            write_x(self.settle_path, dict(rec, settled=ok))
   695	            rec["settled"] = ok
   696	        except BaseException as exc:
   697	            rec["settle_receipt_error"] = repr(exc)
   698	        if not rec["settled"]:
   699	            latch("child %d %s not settled" % (self.seq, self.label))
   700	        self.settled_rec = rec
   701	        return rec
   702	
   703	
   704	def segments(path):
   705	    with open(path, "rb") as f:
   706	        txt = f.read().decode("utf-8", "replace")
   707	    return [s for s in re.split(r"[\r\n]", txt) if s != ""]

   710	def supervise(child, sink):
   711	    """Monitor until exit or a single trip; any monitor exception is itself a trip."""

   713	    last_rss = last_reg = 0.0
   714	    peak = 0
   715	    while child.alive():
   716	        try:
   717	            now = time.time()
   718	            if cancelled():
   719	                child.term("owner_cancel_sigterm")
   720	            elif now > child.deadline:
   721	                child.term("absolute_or_wall_deadline")
   722	            elif sink is not None and sink.over_cap:
   723	                child.term("proof_cap")
   724	            elif sink is not None and sink.error:
   725	                child.term("proof_sink_error")
   726	            elif child.over_cap:
   727	                child.term("capture_cap")
   728	            elif free_bytes() < FREE_FLOOR:
   729	                child.term("inflight_disk_floor")
   730	            if child.term_sent_at is None and now - last_rss >= 1.0:

   710	def supervise(child, sink):
   711	    """Monitor until exit or a single trip; any monitor exception is itself a trip."""
   712	    me = os.getpid()
   713	    last_rss = last_reg = 0.0
   714	    peak = 0
   715	    while child.alive():
   716	        try:
   717	            now = time.time()
   718	            if cancelled():
   719	                child.term("owner_cancel_sigterm")
   720	            elif now > child.deadline:
   721	                child.term("absolute_or_wall_deadline")
   722	            elif sink is not None and sink.over_cap:
   723	                child.term("proof_cap")
   724	            elif sink is not None and sink.error:
   725	                child.term("proof_sink_error")
   726	            elif child.over_cap:
   727	                child.term("capture_cap")
   728	            elif free_bytes() < FREE_FLOOR:
   729	                child.term("inflight_disk_floor")
   730	            if child.term_sent_at is None and now - last_rss >= 1.0:
   731	                last_rss = now
   732	                found, rss = child.census()
   733	                rows = ps_table()

   735	                if found:
   736	                    child.descendants.extend(found)
   737	                    child.term("descendant_process")
   738	                total = rss + runner_rss
   739	                peak = max(peak, total)
   740	                if runner_rss == 0 or (rss == 0 and child.alive()):
   741	                    child.term("rss_unreadable")
   742	                elif total > LANE_RSS_TRIP:
   743	                    child.term("lane_rss")
   744	            if child.term_sent_at is None and now - last_reg >= 5.0:
   745	                last_reg = now
   746	                if registry_refusals():
   747	                    child.term("stop_registry")
   748	        except Exception as exc:
   749	            child.term("monitor_exception:%r" % exc)
   750	        if child.term_sent_at is not None:
   751	            break
   752	        time.sleep(0.25)
   753	    if child.stop is not None and child.stop not in NONLATCHING_TRIPS:
   754	        latch("trip %s on %s" % (child.stop, child.label))
   755	    return child.stop, peak

   757	
   758	def run_child(argv, label, wall, reserve, inputs, sink=None):
   759	    """Prepared outputs -> admission -> spawn -> supervise -> settle.  Returns (rec|None, lines, refusal)."""
   760	    cwd = os.path.join(STATE["out"], "cwd", "%02d-%s" % (STATE["seq"] + 1, label))
   761	    os.makedirs(cwd)
   762	    ch = None
   763	    try:
   764	        ch = Child(argv, label, wall, reserve, inputs, sink, cwd)
   765	        if ch.refused:
   766	            return None, [], ch.refused
   767	        peak = None
   768	        try:
   769	            if cancelled():
   770	                ch.term("owner_cancel_sigterm")
   771	            _, peak = supervise(ch, sink)
   772	        except BaseException as exc:
   773	            latch("runner exception during supervision %r" % exc)
   774	            ch.term("runner_exception")
   775	        rec = ch.settle()
   776	        rec["trip"] = ch.stop
   777	        rec["peak_sampled_lane_rss_bytes"] = peak
   778	        lines = segments(ch.out_path) if rec.get("stream_thread_alive_after_join") is False else []
   779	        return rec, lines, []
   780	    finally:
   781	        for c in OWNED:
   782	            if c.proc is not None and c.settled_rec is None:
   783	                c.settle()  # any escape above still reaps and settles every owned child
   784	        if sink is not None and (ch is None or ch.proc is None):
   785	            sink.finish()  # never-spawned producer: unblock and join the reader
   786

   788	def run_kissat(cnf_path, proof_file, label, seconds, cap, reserve, inputs):
   789	    argv = [KISSAT[0], "-q", "-f", "--seed=1", "--time=%d" % seconds, cnf_path]
   790	    sink = None
   791	    if proof_file is not None:
   792	        fifo_dir = os.path.join(STATE["out"], "fifo")
   793	        os.makedirs(fifo_dir, exist_ok=True)
   794	        sink = ProofSink(os.path.join(fifo_dir, label + ".proof.fifo"), proof_file, cap)
   795	        argv.append(sink.fifo_path)
   796	    return run_child(argv, label + ".kissat", seconds, reserve, dict(inputs, proof_file=proof_file, proof_cap=cap), sink)

   799	def run_drat(cnf_path, proof_path, label, seconds, reserve, inputs):
   800	    return run_child([DRAT, cnf_path, proof_path, "-i", "-t", str(seconds)], label + ".drat", seconds, reserve,
   801	                     dict(inputs, cnf=cnf_path, proof=proof_path))

   804	def sink_complete(sink_rec):
   805	    return (sink_rec is not None and sink_rec.get("sink_settled") and sink_rec["connected"] and sink_rec["eof"]
   806	            and not sink_rec["over_cap"] and sink_rec["error"] is None and not sink_rec["unblocked_by_runner"]
   807	            and sink_rec["discarded"] == 0 and sink_rec["received"] == sink_rec["written"]
   808	            and sink_rec.get("file_rehash") == [sink_rec["sha256_of_written"], sink_rec["written"]])

   811	def status_lines(lines):
   812	    return [s[:200] for s in lines if s.startswith("s ")][:8]
   813	
   814	
   815	def qualify_unsat(cnf_expect, solver_rec, kissat_lines, proof_sha, proof_bytes, proof_cap, drat_rec, drat_lines,
   816	                  cnf_post, proof_post):
   817	    bad = []
   818	    if solver_rec["returncode"] != 20 or solver_rec["stop"] != "self":
   819	        bad.append("solver not self-exit rc20")
   820	    if status_lines(kissat_lines) != ["s UNSATISFIABLE"]:
   821	        bad.append("solver status lines not exactly ['s UNSATISFIABLE']")
   822	    if not sink_complete(solver_rec.get("proof_sink")):
   823	        bad.append("proof sink incomplete/over cap/error")
   824	    if proof_bytes <= 0 or proof_bytes > proof_cap:
   825	        bad.append("proof size outside (0, cap]")
   826	    if drat_rec["returncode"] != 0 or drat_rec["stop"] != "self":
   827	        bad.append("checker not self-exit rc0")
   828	    if status_lines(drat_lines) != ["s VERIFIED"]:
   829	        bad.append("checker status lines not exactly ['s VERIFIED']")
   830	    v, c = cnf_expect[2].split()[2:4]
   831	    if ("c parsing input formula with %s variables and %s clauses" % (v, c)) not in drat_lines:
   832	        bad.append("checker parse header line missing/mismatched")
   833	    if ("c finished parsing, read %d bytes from proof file" % proof_bytes) not in drat_lines:
   834	        bad.append("checker-consumed proof bytes != proof file bytes")
   835	    for pat in ("early EOF", "clauses less than expected", "trivial UNSAT", "ERROR", "MEMOUT",
   836	                "wrong binary prefix", "no empty clause detected", "NOT VERIFIED", "TIMEOUT"):
   837	        if any(pat in s for s in drat_lines):
   838	            bad.append("checker output contains %r" % pat)
   839	    if cnf_post != (cnf_expect[0], cnf_expect[1]):
   840	        bad.append("CNF file digest/bytes != expected")
   841	    if proof_post != (proof_sha, proof_bytes):
   842	        bad.append("proof file digest/bytes != sink measurement")
   843	    for rec in (solver_rec, drat_rec):
   844	        if not rec["settled"] or rec["capture_over_cap"]:
   845	            bad.append("settlement/capture defect seq %s" % rec["seq"])
   846	    return bad
   847	
   848	
   849	def verify_sat(cnf_path, kissat_lines, label):
   850	    """Two independent checks of a SAT model: clause satisfaction and both PA19 definitions."""
   851	    lits = set()
   852	    for s in kissat_lines:
   853	        if s.startswith("v "):
   854	            lits.update(int(x) for x in s[2:].split() if x != "0")
   855	    sat_all = True
   856	    with open(cnf_path, "rb") as f:
   857	        for ln in f:
   858	            if ln.startswith(b"p") or ln.startswith(b"c"):
   859	                continue
   860	            cl = [int(x) for x in ln.split()[:-1]]
   861	            if not any(l in lits for l in cl):
   862	                sat_all = False
   863	                break
   864	    A = [[None] * 8 for _ in range(19)]
   865	    cells_ok = True
   866	    for r in range(19):
   867	        for c in range(8):
   868	            ss = [s for s in range(6) if (1 + 48 * r + 6 * c + s) in lits]
   869	            if len(ss) != 1:
   870	                cells_ok = False
   871	            else:
   872	                A[r][c] = ss[0]
   873	    rowpair_ok = cells_ok and all(sum(A[r][c] == A[q][c] for c in range(8)) <= 1
   874	                                  for r in range(19) for q in range(r + 1, 19))
   875	    colpair_ok = cells_ok and all(len({(A[r][c], A[r][d]) for r in range(19)}) == 19
   876	                                  for c in range(8) for d in range(c + 1, 8))
   877	    cand = os.path.join(STATE["out"], label + ".SAT-CANDIDATE.json")
   878	    write_x(cand, {"host_clock": clock(), "array": A, "clauses_all_satisfied": sat_all,
   879	                   "cells_exactly_one": cells_ok, "row_pair_agreements_le_1": rowpair_ok,
   880	                   "column_pair_ordered_pairs_distinct": colpair_ok,
   881	                   "status": "CANDIDATE_UNVERIFIED - notify parent immediately"})
   882	    latch_path = os.path.join(LANE, "control", "SAT-CANDIDATE-" + label)
   883	    write_bytes_x(latch_path, cand.encode())
   884	    return {"candidate": cand, "latch": latch_path, "clauses_all_satisfied": sat_all, "cells": cells_ok,
   885	            "rowpair": rowpair_ok, "colpair": colpair_ok}
   886	
   887	
   888	def php_cnf(p, h):
   889	    """Pigeonhole: p pigeons into h holes; UNSAT iff p > h."""
   890	    var = lambda i, j: i * h + j + 1  # noqa: E731
   891	    cl = [[var(i, j) for j in range(h)] for i in range(p)]
   892	    for j in range(h):
   893	        for i in range(p):
   894	            for k in range(i + 1, p):
   895	                cl.append([-var(i, j), -var(k, j)])
   896	    return ("p cnf %d %d\n" % (p * h, len(cl)) + "".join(" ".join(map(str, c)) + " 0\n" for c in cl)).encode()
   897	
   898	
   899	def healthy(rec):
   900	    """A control child must have exited by itself (rc>=0), with no trip, fully settled, no resource diag."""
   901	    return rec is not None and rec["stop"] == "self" and rec["settled"] and not rec["capture_over_cap"] \
   902	        and rec["returncode"] is not None and rec["returncode"] >= 0 and not rec["resource_diagnostics"]

   904	
   905	def completed_checker(rec, lines, expect):
   906	    """r3#8: a completed drat-trim outcome: exactly 's VERIFIED' rc0 or 's NOT VERIFIED' rc1, no
   907	    MEMOUT/TIMEOUT/ERROR text, healthy settlement."""
   908	    st = status_lines(lines)
   909	    if not healthy(rec) or any(p in s for s in lines for p in ("MEMOUT", "TIMEOUT", "ERROR")):
   910	        return False
   911	    if expect == "VERIFIED":
   912	        return st == ["s VERIFIED"] and rec["returncode"] == 0
   913	    return st == ["s NOT VERIFIED"] and rec["returncode"] == 1

   916	def controls():
   917	    """Same-binary positive and intended-reason mutation controls; stops at the first refused,
   918	    unhealthy or not-as-intended control (later controls are not started)."""
   919	    recs = {}

  1030	def terminal_gate():
  1031	    """Registry and disk evidence collected BEFORE the commit boundary; any failure latches."""
  1032	    why = []
  1033	    try:
  1034	        why += registry_refusals()
  1035	        if free_bytes() < FREE_FLOOR:
  1036	            why.append("free below in-flight floor at terminal gate")
  1037	    except Exception as exc:
  1038	        why.append("terminal gate exception %r" % exc)
  1039	    for w in why:
  1040	        latch("terminal gate: " + w)
  1041	    return why
  1042	
  1043	
  1044	def commit_record(path, rec_unknown, rec_unsat):
  1045	    """Explicit commit boundary.  Both serializations and the O_EXCL fd exist before the boundary; the
  1046	    boundary is one atomic next(ORDER); a cancel stamped before it (or any latch) forces UNKNOWN."""
  1047	    unknown_bytes = serialize(rec_unknown)
  1048	    unsat_bytes = None if rec_unsat is None else serialize(rec_unsat)
  1049	    fd = os.open(path, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL, 0o644)
  1050	    try:
  1051	        boundary = next(ORDER)
  1052	        co = STATE["cancel_order"]
  1053	        chosen = unsat_bytes if (unsat_bytes is not None and not LATCH and (co is None or co > boundary)) else unknown_bytes
  1054	        view = memoryview(chosen)
  1055	        while view:
  1056	            k = os.write(fd, view)
  1057	            view = view[k:]
  1058	        os.fsync(fd)
  1059	    finally:
  1060	        os.close(fd)
  1061	    return {"boundary": boundary, "cancel_order": STATE["cancel_order"], "committed_unsat": chosen is unsat_bytes,
  1062	            "sha256": hashlib.sha256(chosen).hexdigest()}
  1063

  1138	def run_plan(summary, outcome):
  1139	    """The plan body; every early stop is a plain return with the outcome flags set."""
  1140	    out_dir = STATE["out"]
  1141	    if sha_file(DRAT_OLD[0])[0] != DRAT_OLD[1] or sha_file(KISSAT[0])[0] != KISSAT[1]:
  1142	        raise RuntimeError("binary pin mismatch")
  1143	    if sha_file(DRAT)[0] != DRAT_OLD[1] or os.lstat(DRAT).st_ino == os.lstat(DRAT_OLD[0]).st_ino:
  1144	        raise RuntimeError("vendored drat-trim mismatch or shared inode")
  1145	    ok, recs = controls()
  1146	    summary["controls_ok"] = ok
  1147	    summary["controls_record_sha256"] = write_x(os.path.join(out_dir, "controls.json"), recs)
  1148	    STATE["remaining_reserve"] -= CONTROL_RESERVE
  1149	    if not ok:
  1150	        outcome["controls_failed"] = True
  1151	        summary["stopped"] = {"phase": "controls"}
  1152	        return
  1153	    read_pinned(ENC[0], ENC[1])
  1154	    sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
  1155	    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("encode_skel_b_lane", ENC[0])
  1156	    E = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
  1157	    spec.loader.exec_module(E)
  1158	    D = json.loads(read_pinned(*SKEL))
  1159	    special = [p for p in D["profiles"] if list(p) != P0]
  1160	    for it in ITEMS:
  1161	        label = it["id"].replace(":", "_")
  1162	        rec = {"id": it["id"], "status": "UNKNOWN", "count_for_scientific_coverage": False,
  1163	               "host_clock_start": clock()}
  1164	        rec_path = os.path.join(out_dir, label + ".record.json")
  1165	        Ej = json.loads(read_pinned(*it["list"]))
  1166	        if not (Ej["num_placements"] == len(Ej["placements"]) == it["denominator"]):
  1167	            raise RuntimeError("list denominator mismatch " + it["id"])
  1168	        sk = D["skeletons"][it["b"]]
  1169	        nv, cl = E.build_partbatch(D["c0"], [tuple(t) for t in sk["types"]], sk["empty"], special,
  1170	                                   [Ej["placements"][i] for i in range(it["lo"], it["hi"] + 1)])
  1171	        cnf = E.cnf_bytes(nv, cl)
  1172	        del cl
  1173	        got = (hashlib.sha256(cnf).hexdigest(), len(cnf), cnf.split(b"\n", 1)[0].decode())
  1174	        if got != it["cnf"]:
  1175	            raise RuntimeError("CNF digest mismatch %r" % (got,))
  1176	        cnf_path = os.path.join(out_dir, label + ".cnf")
  1177	        write_bytes_x(cnf_path, cnf)
  1178	        del cnf
  1179	        if sha_file(cnf_path) != (it["cnf"][0], it["cnf"][1]):
  1180	            raise RuntimeError("CNF file re-hash mismatch")
  1181	        inputs = {"item": it["id"], "cnf_pin": [it["cnf"][0], it["cnf"][1]], "list_pin": it["list"][1],
  1182	                  "skeleton_pin": SKEL[1], "encoder_pin": ENC[1]}
  1183	        proof_path = os.path.join(out_dir, label + ".drat")
  1184	        ks, kl, refused = run_kissat(cnf_path, proof_path, label, SOLVER_SECONDS, PROOF_CAP,
  1185	                                     PROOF_CAP + LOG_CAP + 3 * RECORD_MAX, inputs)
  1186	        if ks is None:
  1187	            rec.update(reason="solver admission refused", refusal=refused, host_clock_end=clock())
  1188	            write_x(rec_path, rec)
  1189	            summary["items"][it["id"]] = {"status": "UNKNOWN", "reason": "solver admission refused"}
  1190	            summary["stopped"] = {"phase": "solver_admission", "item": it["id"], "why": refused}
  1191	            outcome["refused"] = True
  1192	            return
  1193	        rec["solver"] = ks
  1194	        rec["solver_status_lines"] = status_lines(kl)
  1195	        if ks["returncode"] == 10 and rec["solver_status_lines"] == ["s SATISFIABLE"] and ks["stop"] == "self" \
  1196	                and ks["settled"]:

  1200	            write_x(rec_path, rec)
  1201	            summary["items"][it["id"]] = {"status": "SAT_CANDIDATE", "sat": rec["sat"]}
  1202	            summary["stopped"] = {"phase": "sat_candidate", "item": it["id"]}
  1203	            outcome["sat"] = True
  1204	            return
  1205	        sink_ok = sink_complete(ks["proof_sink"])
  1206	        if sink_ok:
  1207	            rec["proof"] = {"sha256": ks["proof_sink"]["sha256_of_written"], "bytes": ks["proof_sink"]["written"]}
  1208	        if ks["returncode"] == 20 and ks["stop"] == "self" and ks["settled"] and sink_ok and not LATCH \
  1209	                and not cancelled():
  1210	            dr, dl, refused = run_drat(cnf_path, proof_path, label, CHECK_SECONDS, LOG_CAP + 3 * RECORD_MAX,
  1211	                                       dict(inputs, proof_pin=[rec["proof"]["sha256"], rec["proof"]["bytes"]]))
  1212	            if dr is None:
  1213	                rec.update(reason="checker admission refused", refusal=refused, host_clock_end=clock())
  1214	                write_x(rec_path, rec)
  1215	                summary["items"][it["id"]] = {"status": "UNKNOWN", "reason": "checker admission refused"}
  1216	                summary["stopped"] = {"phase": "checker_admission", "item": it["id"], "why": refused}
  1217	                outcome["refused"] = True
  1218	                return
  1219	            rec["checker"] = dr
  1220	            rec["checker_status_lines"] = status_lines(dl)
  1221	            rec["checker_parse_lines"] = [s[:200] for s in dl if s.startswith("c parsing")

+def bounded(x):
+    """r3#7: bound strings/lists recursively, replacing removed text by its SHA-256."""
+    if isinstance(x, str):
+        if len(x) <= STR_MAX:
+            return x
+        return x[:STR_MAX] + "...[truncated %d chars sha256 %s]" % (len(x), hashlib.sha256(x.encode()).hexdigest())
+    if isinstance(x, (list, tuple)):
+        y = [bounded(v) for v in x[:LIST_MAX]]
+        if len(x) > LIST_MAX:
+            y.append("...[%d more items]" % (len(x) - LIST_MAX))
+        return y
+    if isinstance(x, dict):
+        return {str(k)[:200]: bounded(v) for k, v in x.items()}
+    return x
+
+
+def serialize(obj):
+    data = json.dumps(bounded(obj), indent=1, sort_keys=True).encode()
+    if len(data) > RECORD_MAX:
+        raise RuntimeError("record exceeds RECORD_MAX (%d bytes)" % len(data))
+    return data
+
+
+def write_x(path, obj):
+    data = serialize(obj)
+    with open(path, "xb") as f:
+        f.write(data)
+        f.flush()
+        os.fsync(f.fileno())
+    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
+
+
+def write_bytes_x(path, data):
+    with open(path, "xb") as f:
+        f.write(data)
+        f.flush()
+        os.fsync(f.fileno())
+    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest(), len(data)

    92	static inline void removeWatch (struct solver* S, int* clause, int index) {
    93	  int i, lit = clause[index];
    94	  if ((S->used[lit] > INIT) && (S->max[lit] > 2 * S->used[lit])) {
    95	    S->max[lit] = (3 * S->used[lit]) >> 1;

    97	    assert(S->wlist[lit] != NULL); }
    98	  long *watch = S->wlist[lit];
    99	  for (i = 0; i < S->used[lit]; i++) {

   114	static inline void removeUnit (struct solver* S, int lit) {

   119	  S->unitSize--; }

   121	static inline void unassignUnit (struct solver* S, int lit) {
   122	  if (S->verb)
   123	    printf ("\rc removing unit %i\n", lit);
   124	  while (S->falseA[-lit]) {
   125	    if (S->verb)
   126	      printf ("\rc removing unit %i (%i)\n", S->forced[-1], lit);
   127	    S->falseA[*(--S->forced)] = 0;
   128	    S->reason[abs(*S->forced)] = 0; }
   129	  S->processed = S->assigned = S->forced; }
   130	
   131	static inline void markWatch (struct solver* S, int* clause, int index, int offset) {
   132	  long* watch = S->wlist[ clause[ index ] ];
   133	  for (;;) {
   134	    int *_clause = (S->DB + (*(watch++) >> 1) + (long) offset);
   135	    if (_clause == clause) { watch[ID] |= ACTIVE; return; } } }
   136	
   137	static inline void addDependency (struct solver* S, int dep, int forced) {
   138	  if (1 || S->traceFile || S->lratFile) { // temporary for MAXDEP
   139	    if (S->nDependencies == S->maxDependencies) {
   140	      S->maxDependencies = (S->maxDependencies * 3) >> 1;
   141	//      printf ("c dependencies increased to %i\n", S->maxDependencies);
   142	      S->dependencies = realloc (S->dependencies, sizeof (int) * S->maxDependencies);
   143	      if (S->dependencies == NULL) { printf ("c MEMOUT: dependencies reallocation failed\n"); exit (0); } }
   144	//    printf("c adding dep %i\n", (dep << 1) + forced);
   145	    S->dependencies[S->nDependencies++] = (dep << 1) + forced; } }
   146	
   147	static inline void markClause (struct solver* S, int* clause, int index) {
   148	  S->nResolve++;
   149	  addDependency (S, clause[index - 1] >> 1, (S->assigned > S->forced));
   150	
  1038	        S->proof[step  ] = b;
  1039	        S->proof[step-1] = a; } } }
  1040	
  1041	  for (step = 0; step < S->nStep; step++) {
  1042	    long ad = S->proof[step];
  1043	    if (ad & 1) continue;
  1044	    int *clause = S->DB + (ad >> INFOBITS);
  1045	    int i, length = 0;
  1046	    while (*clause) { length++; clause++; }
  1047	    clause = S->DB + (ad >> INFOBITS);
  1048	    for (i = 0; i < length - 1; i++) {
  1049	      int j = i + rand() / (RAND_MAX / (length - i) + 1);
  1050	      int t = clause[i]; clause[i] = clause[j]; clause[j] = t; } } }
  1051	
  1052	int parse (struct solver* S) {
  1053	  int tmp, active = 0, retvalue = SAT;
  1054	  int del = 0, fileLine = 0;
  1055	  int *buffer, bufferAlloc;
  1056	
  1057	  S->nVars    = 0;
  1058	  S->nClauses = 0;
  1059	  do { tmp = fscanf (S->inputFile, " cnf %i %li \n", &S->nVars, &S->nClauses); // Read the first line
  1060	    if (tmp > 0 && tmp != EOF) break; tmp = fscanf (S->inputFile, "%*s\n"); }  // In case a commment line was found
  1061	  while (tmp != 2 && tmp != EOF);                                              // Skip it and read next line
  1062	  int nZeros = S->nClauses;
  1063	
  1064	  if (!S->nVars && !S->nClauses) {
  1065	    printf ("\rc ERROR: did not find p cnf line in input file\n"); exit (0); }
  1066	
  1067	  printf ("\rc parsing input formula with %i variables and %li clauses\n", S->nVars, S->nClauses);
  1068	
  1069	  bufferAlloc = INIT;
  1070	  buffer = (int*) malloc (sizeof (int) * bufferAlloc);
  1071	
  1072	  S->count    = 1;
  1073	  S->nStep    = 0;
  1074	  S->mem_used = 0;                  // The number of integers allocated in the DB
  1075	
  1076	  long size;
  1077	  long DBsize = S->mem_used + BIGINIT;
  1078	  S->DB = (int*) malloc (DBsize * sizeof (int));
  1079	  if (S->DB == NULL) { free (buffer); return ERROR; }
  1080	
  1081	  S->maxVar  = 0;
  1082	  S->maxSize = 0;
  1083	  S->nLemmas = 0;
  1084	  S->nAlloc  = BIGINIT;
  1085	  S->formula = (long *) malloc (sizeof (long) * S->nClauses);
  1086	  S->proof   = (long *) malloc (sizeof (long) * S->nAlloc);
  1087	  long **hashTable = (long**) malloc (sizeof (long*) * BIGINIT);
  1088	  int   *hashUsed  = (int * ) malloc (sizeof (int  ) * BIGINIT);
  1089	  int   *hashMax   = (int * ) malloc (sizeof (int  ) * BIGINIT);
  1090	
  1091	  int i;
  1092	  for (i = 0; i < BIGINIT; i++) {
  1093	    hashUsed [i] = 0;
  1094	    hashMax  [i] = INIT;
  1095	    hashTable[i] = (long*) malloc (sizeof (long) * hashMax[i]); }
  1096	
  1097	  int fileSwitchFlag = 0;
  1098	  long finalClause = 0;
  1099	  size = 0;
  1100	  while (1) {
  1101	    int lit = 0; tmp = 0;
  1102	    fileSwitchFlag = nZeros <= 0;
  1103	
  1104	    if (size == 0) {
  1105	      if (fileSwitchFlag) { // read for proof
  1106	        if (S->binMode == 1) {
  1107	          int res = getc_unlocked (S->proofFile);
  1108	          if      (res == EOF) break;
  1109	          else if (res ==  97) del = 0;
  1110	          else if (res == 100) del = 1;
  1111	          else {
  1112	            if (S->warning != NOWARNING) {
  1113	              printf ("\rc WARNING: wrong binary prefix, stop reading proof\n"); break; }
  1114	            if (S->warning == HARDWARNING) exit (HARDWARNING); }
  1115	          S->nReads++; }
  1116	        else {
  1117	          tmp = fscanf (S->proofFile, " d  %i ", &lit);
  1118	          if (tmp == EOF) break;
  1119	          del = tmp > 0; } } }
  1120	
  1121	    if (!lit) {
  1122	      if (!fileSwitchFlag) tmp = fscanf (S->inputFile, " %i ", &lit);  // Read a literal.
  1123	      else {
  1124	        if (S->binMode == 1) {
  1125	          tmp = read_lit (S, &lit); }
  1126	        else {
  1127	          tmp = fscanf (S->proofFile, " %i ", &lit); } }
  1128	      if (tmp == EOF && !fileSwitchFlag) {
  1129	        if (S->warning != NOWARNING) {
  1130	          printf ("\rc WARNING: early EOF of the input formula\n");
  1131	          printf ("\rc WARNING: %i clauses less than expected\n", nZeros); }
  1132	        if (S->warning == HARDWARNING) exit (HARDWARNING);
  1133	        fileLine = 0;
  1134	        fileSwitchFlag = 1; } }
  1135	
  1136	    if (!tmp && !S->binMode) {
  1137	      char ignore[1<<16];
  1138	      if (!fileSwitchFlag) { if (fgets (ignore, sizeof (ignore), S->inputFile) == NULL) printf ("c\n"); }
  1139	      else                   if (fgets (ignore, sizeof (ignore), S->proofFile) == NULL) printf ("c\n");
  1140	      if (ignore[0] != 'c') continue;
  1141	      for (i = 0; i < sizeof ignore; i++) { if (ignore[i] == '\n') break; }
  1142	      if (i == sizeof ignore) {
  1143	        printf ("c ERROR: comment longer than %zu characters: %s\n", sizeof ignore, ignore);
  1144	        exit (HARDWARNING); }
  1145	      if (S->verb) printf ("\rc WARNING: parsing mismatch assuming a comment\n");
  1146	      continue; }
  1147	
  1148	    if (abs (lit) > S->maxVar) S->maxVar = abs (lit);
  1149	    if (tmp == EOF && fileSwitchFlag) break;
  1150	    if (abs (lit) > S->nVars && !fileSwitchFlag) {
  1360	int binChar (int c) {
  1361	  // ASCII characters: 10 (LF), 13 (CR), 32 (' '), 45 ('-'), 48 ('0'), 57 ('9'), 99 ('c'), 100 ('d')
  1362	  if ((c != 10) && (c != 13) && (c != 32) && (c != 45) && ((c < 48) || (c > 57)) && (c != 99) && (c != 100))
  1363	    return 1;
  1364	  return 0;
  1365	}
  1366	
  1367	int main (int argc, char** argv) {
  1368	  struct solver S;
  1369	
  1370	  S.inputFile  = NULL;
  1371	  S.proofFile  = stdin;
  1372	  S.coreStr    = NULL;
  1373	  S.activeFile = NULL;
  1374	  S.lemmaStr   = NULL;
  1375	  S.lratFile   = NULL;
  1376	  S.traceFile  = NULL;
  1377	  S.timeout    = TIMEOUT;
  1378	  S.nReads     = 0;
  1379	  S.nWrites    = 0;
  1380	  S.mask       = 0;
  1381	  S.verb       = 0;
  1382	  S.delProof   = 0;
  1383	  S.backforce  = 0;
  1384	  S.optimize   = 0;
  1385	  S.warning    = 0;
  1386	  S.prep       = 0;
  1387	  S.bar        = 0;
  1388	  S.mode       = BACKWARD_UNSAT;
  1389	  S.delete     = 1;
  1390	  S.reduce     = 1;
  1391	  S.binMode    = 0;
  1392	  S.binOutput  = 0;
  1393	  S.rupOnly    = 0;
  1394	  gettimeofday (&S.start_time, NULL);
  1395	
  1396	  int i, tmp = 0;
  1397	  for (i = 1; i < argc; i++) {
  1398	    if        (argv[i][0] == '-') {
  1399	      if      (argv[i][1] == 'h') printHelp ();
  1400	      else if (argv[i][1] == 'c') S.coreStr    = argv[++i];
  1401	      else if (argv[i][1] == 'a') S.activeFile = fopen (argv[++i], "w");
  1402	      else if (argv[i][1] == 'l') S.lemmaStr   = argv[++i];
  1403	      else if (argv[i][1] == 'L') S.lratFile   = fopen (argv[++i], "w");
  1404	      else if (argv[i][1] == 'r') S.traceFile  = fopen (argv[++i], "w");
  1405	      else if (argv[i][1] == 't') S.timeout    = atoi (argv[++i]);
  1406	      else if (argv[i][1] == 'b') S.bar        = 1;
  1407	      else if (argv[i][1] == 'B') S.backforce  = 1;
  1408	      else if (argv[i][1] == 'O') S.optimize   = 1;
  1409	      else if (argv[i][1] == 'C') S.binOutput  = 1;
  1410	      else if (argv[i][1] == 'D') S.delProof   = 1;
  1411	      else if (argv[i][1] == 'i') S.binMode    = 1;
  1412	      else if (argv[i][1] == 'I') S.binMode    = -1;
  1413	      else if (argv[i][1] == 'u') S.mask       = 1;
  1414	      else if (argv[i][1] == 'v') S.verb       = 1;
  1415	      else if (argv[i][1] == 'w') S.warning    = NOWARNING;
  1416	      else if (argv[i][1] == 'W') S.warning    = HARDWARNING;
  1417	      else if (argv[i][1] == 'p') S.delete     = 0;
  1418	      else if (argv[i][1] == 'R') S.reduce     = 0;
  1419	      else if (argv[i][1] == 'f') S.mode       = FORWARD_UNSAT;
  1420	      else if (argv[i][1] == 'S') S.mode       = FORWARD_SAT;
  1421	      else if (argv[i][1] == 'U') S.rupOnly    = 1; }
  1422	    else {
  1423	      tmp++;
  1424	      if (tmp == 1) {
  1425	        S.inputFile = fopen (argv[1], "r");
  1426	        if (S.inputFile == NULL) {
  1427	          printf ("\rc error opening \"%s\".\n", argv[i]); return ERROR; } }
  1428	
  1429	      else if (tmp == 2) {
  1430	        S.proofFile = fopen (argv[2], "r");
  1431	        if (S.proofFile == NULL) {
  1432	          printf ("\rc error opening \"%s\".\n", argv[i]); return ERROR; }
  1433	
  1434	       int c, comment = 1;
  1435	       if (S.binMode == 0) {
  1436	          c = getc_unlocked (S.proofFile); // check the first character in the file
  1437	          if (c == EOF) { S.binMode = 1; continue; }
  1438	          if (binChar (c)) {
  1439	             printf ("\rc turning on binary mode checking\n");
  1440	             S.binMode = 1; }
  1441	          if (c != 99) comment = 0; }
  1442	        if (S.binMode == 0) {
  1443	          c = getc_unlocked (S.proofFile); // check the second character in the file
  1444	          if (c == EOF) { S.binMode = 1; continue; }
  1445	          if (binChar (c)) {
  1446	             printf ("\rc turning on binary mode checking\n");
  1447	             S.binMode = 1; }
  1448	          if (c != 32) comment = 0; }
  1449	        if (S.binMode == 0) {
  1450	          int j;
  1451	          for (j = 0; j < 10; j++) {
  1452	            c = getc_unlocked (S.proofFile);
  1453	            if (c == EOF) break;
  1454	            if (binChar (c) && (!comment || c < 65 || c > 122)) {
  1455	              printf ("\rc turning on binary mode checking\n");
  1456	              S.binMode = 1; break; } } }
  1457	        fclose (S.proofFile);
  1458	        if (S.binMode == -1) S.binMode = 0;
  1459	        S.proofFile = fopen (argv[2], "r");
  1460	        if (S.proofFile == NULL) {
  1461	          printf ("\rc error opening \"%s\".\n", argv[i]); return ERROR; } } } }
  1462	
  1463	  if (tmp == 1) printf ("\rc reading proof from stdin\n");
  1464	  if (tmp == 0) printHelp ();
  1465	
  1466	  int parseReturnValue = parse (&S);
  1467	
  1468	  fclose (S.inputFile);
  1469	  fclose (S.proofFile);
  1470	
  1471	  if (S.mode == FORWARD_UNSAT) {
  1472	    S.reduce = 0; }
  1473	
  1474	  if (S.delProof && argv[2] != NULL) {
  1475	    int ret = remove(argv[2]);
  1476	    if (ret == 0) printf("c deleted proof %s\n", argv[2]); }
  1477	
  1478	  int sts = ERROR;
  1479	  if       (parseReturnValue == ERROR)          printf ("\rs MEMORY ALLOCATION ERROR\n");
  1480	  else if  (parseReturnValue == UNSAT)          printf ("\rc trivial UNSAT\ns VERIFIED\n");
  1481	  else if  ((sts = verify (&S, -1, -1)) == UNSAT) printf ("\rs VERIFIED\n");
  1482	  else if  (sts == DERIVATION)                  printf ("\rs DERIVATION\n");
  1483	  else printf ("\ns NOT VERIFIED\n")  ;
  1484	  struct timeval current_time;
  1485	  gettimeofday (&current_time, NULL);
  1486	  long runtime = (current_time.tv_sec  - S.start_time.tv_sec) * 1000000 +
  1487	                 (current_time.tv_usec - S.start_time.tv_usec);
  1488	  printf ("\rc verification time: %.3f seconds\n", (double) (runtime / 1000000.0));
  1489	
  1490	  if (S.optimize) {
  1491	    printf("c proof optimization started (ignoring the timeout)\n");
  1492	    int iteration = 1;
  1493	    while (S.nRemoved && iteration < 10000) {
  1494	      deactivate (&S);
  1495	      shuffleProof (&S, iteration);
  1496	      iteration++;
  1497	      verify (&S, 0, 0); } }
  1498	
  1499	  freeMemory (&S);
  1500	  return (sts != UNSAT); // 0 on success, 1 on any failure
  1501	}

   126	static int bz2sig[] = {0x42, 0x5A, 0x68, EOF};
   127	static int gzsig[] = {0x1F, 0x8B, EOF};
   128	static int lzmasig[] = {0x5D, 0x00, 0x00, 0x80, 0x00, EOF};

   133	static bool match_signature (const char *path, const int *sig) {
   134	  assert (path);
   135	  FILE *tmp = fopen (path, "r");
   136	  if (!tmp)
   137	    return false;
   138	  bool res = true;
   139	  for (const int *p = sig; res && (*p != EOF); p++)
   140	    res = (getc (tmp) == *p);
   141	  fclose (tmp);
   142	  return res;
   143	}

   145	#ifdef KISSAT_HAS_COMPRESSION
   146	
   147	static FILE *open_pipe (const char *fmt, const char *path,
   148	                        const char *mode) {
   149	  size_t name_len = 0;
   150	  while (fmt[name_len] && fmt[name_len] != ' ')
   151	    name_len++;
   152	  char *name = malloc (name_len + 1);
   153	  if (!name)
   154	    return 0;
   155	  strncpy (name, fmt, name_len);
   156	  name[name_len] = 0;
   157	  bool found = kissat_find_executable (name);
   158	  free (name);
   159	  if (!found)
   160	    return 0;
   161	  char *cmd = malloc (strlen (fmt) + strlen (path));
   162	  if (!cmd)
   163	    return 0;
   164	  sprintf (cmd, fmt, path);
   165	  FILE *res = popen (cmd, mode);
   166	  free (cmd);
   167	  return res;
   168	}
   169	
   170	static FILE *read_pipe (const char *fmt, const int *sig, const char *path) {
   171	  if (!kissat_file_readable (path))
   172	    return 0;
   173	  if (sig && !match_signature (path, sig))
   174	    return 0;
   175	  return open_pipe (fmt, path, "r");
   176	}

   181	  return open_pipe (fmt, path, "w");
   182	}

   184	#endif

   209	bool kissat_looks_like_a_compressed_file (const char *path) {
   210	#define RETURN_TRUE_IF_COMPRESSED(SUFFIX, SIGNATURE) \
   211	  if (kissat_has_suffix (path, SUFFIX) && \
   212	      match_signature (path, SIGNATURE)) \
   213	  return true
   214	
   215	  RETURN_TRUE_IF_COMPRESSED (".bz2", bz2sig);
   216	  RETURN_TRUE_IF_COMPRESSED (".gz", gzsig);
   217	  RETURN_TRUE_IF_COMPRESSED (".lzma", lzmasig);
   218	  RETURN_TRUE_IF_COMPRESSED (".7z", sig7z);
   219	  RETURN_TRUE_IF_COMPRESSED (".xz", xzsig);
   220	  RETURN_TRUE_IF_COMPRESSED (".Z", Zsig);

   223	}
