   227	bool kissat_open_to_read_file (file *file, const char *path) {
   228	#ifdef KISSAT_HAS_COMPRESSION
   229	#define READ_PIPE(SUFFIX, CMD, SIG) \
   230	  do { \
   231	    if (kissat_has_suffix (path, SUFFIX)) { \
   232	      file->file = read_pipe (CMD, SIG, path); \
   233	      if (!file->file) \
   234	        break; \
   235	      file->close = true; \
   236	      file->reading = true; \
   237	      file->compressed = true; \
   238	      file->path = path; \
   239	      file->bytes = 0; \
   240	      return true; \
   241	    } \
   242	  } while (0)
   243	  READ_PIPE (".bz2", "bzip2 -c -d %s", bz2sig);
   244	  READ_PIPE (".gz", "gzip -c -d %s", gzsig);
   245	  READ_PIPE (".lzma", "lzma -c -d %s", lzmasig);
   246	  READ_PIPE (".7z", "7z x -so %s 2>/dev/null", sig7z);
   247	  READ_PIPE (".xz", "xz -c -d %s", xzsig);
   248	  READ_PIPE (".Z", "gzip -c -d %s", Zsig);
   249	#endif
   250	  file->file = fopen (path, "r");
   251	  if (!file->file)
   252	    return false;
   253	  file->close = true;
   254	  file->reading = true;
   255	  file->compressed = false;
   256	  file->path = path;
   257	  file->bytes = 0;
   258	
   259	  return true;
   260	}
   261	
   262	bool kissat_open_to_write_file (file *file, const char *path) {
   263	#if defined(KISSAT_HAS_COMPRESSION) && !defined(SAFE)
   264	#define WRITE_PIPE(SUFFIX, CMD) \
   265	  do { \
   266	    if (kissat_has_suffix (path, SUFFIX)) { \
   267	      if (SUFFIX[1] == '7' && kissat_file_readable (path) && \
   268	          unlink (path)) \
   269	        return false; \
   270	      file->file = write_pipe (CMD, path); \
   271	      if (!file->file) \
   272	        return false; \
   273	      file->close = true; \
   274	      file->reading = false; \
   275	      file->compressed = true; \
   276	      file->path = path; \
   277	      file->bytes = 0; \
   278	      return true; \
   279	    } \
   280	  } while (0)
   281	  WRITE_PIPE (".bz2", "bzip2 -c > %s");
   282	  WRITE_PIPE (".gz", "gzip -c > %s");
   283	  WRITE_PIPE (".lzma", "lzma -c > %s");
   284	  WRITE_PIPE (".7z", "7z a -si %s 2>/dev/null");
   285	  WRITE_PIPE (".xz", "xz -c > %s");
   286	#endif
   287	  file->file = fopen (path, "w");
   288	  if (!file->file)
   289	    return false;
   290	  file->close = true;
   291	  file->reading = false;
   292	  file->compressed = false;
   293	  file->path = path;
   294	  file->bytes = 0;
   295	  return true;
   296	}
   297	
   298	void kissat_close_file (file *file) {
   299	  assert (file);
   300	  assert (file->file);
   301	#ifdef KISSAT_HAS_COMPRESSION
   302	  if (file->close && file->compressed)
   303	    pclose (file->file);
   304	#else
   305	  assert (!file->compressed);
   306	#endif
   307	  if (file->close && !file->compressed)
   308	    fclose (file->file);
   309	  file->file = 0;
   310	}
   530	      arg = argv[i];
   531	      if (application->output_path)
   532	        ERROR ("multiple output options '-o %s' and '-o %s' (try '-h')",
   533	               application->output_path, arg);
   534	      application->output_path = arg;
   535	    }
   536	#ifdef NOPTIONS
   537	    else if (arg[0] == '-' && !arg[2] &&
   538	             (arg[1] == 'l' || arg[1] == 'q' || arg[1] == 's' ||
   539	              arg[1] == 'v'))
   540	      ERROR ("invalid short option '%s' "
   541	             "(configured with '--no-options')",
   542	             arg);
   543	#endif
   544	#ifdef QUIET
   545	    else if (arg[0] == '-' && !arg[2] &&
   546	             (arg[1] == 'q' || arg[1] == 's' || arg[1] == 'v'))
   547	      ERROR ("invalid short option '%s' (configured with '-q')", arg);
   548	#endif
   549	#ifndef LOGGING
   550	    else if (!strcmp (arg, "-l"))
   551	      ERROR ("invalid short option '%s' "
   552	             "(configured without '-l' or '-g')",
   553	             arg);
   554	#endif
   555	    else if (arg[0] == '-' && arg[1])
   556	      ERROR ("invalid short option '%s' (try '-h')", arg);
   557	#ifndef NPROOFS
   558	    else if (application->proof_path)
   559	      ERROR ("three file arguments '%s', '%s' and '%s' (try '-h')",
   560	             application->input_path, application->proof_path, arg);
   561	#endif
   562	    else if (application->input_path) {
   563	#ifndef NPROOFS
   564	      const char *input_path = application->input_path;
   565	      if (!strcmp (input_path, arg))
   566	        ERROR ("will not read and write '%s' at the same time", input_path);
   567	#ifdef KISSAT_HAS_COMPRESSION
   568	      {
   569	        char *real_input_path = realpath (input_path, 0);
   570	        if (real_input_path) {
   571	          char *real_arg_path = realpath (arg, 0);
   572	          if (real_arg_path) {
   573	            if (!strcmp (real_input_path, real_arg_path)) {
   574	              if (strcmp (arg, real_arg_path) &&
   575	                  strcmp (input_path, real_input_path))
   576	                ERROR ("will not read and write '%s' and '%s' "
   577	                       "pointing to the same file '%s'",
   578	                       input_path, arg, real_input_path);
   579	              else
   580	                ERROR ("will not read and write '%s' and '%s' "
   581	                       "pointing to the same file",
   582	                       input_path, arg);
   583	            }
   584	            free (real_arg_path);
   585	          }
   586	          free (real_input_path);
   587	        } else
   588	          ERROR ("can not get absolute path of '%s' (unexpectedly)",
   589	                 input_path);
   590	      }
   591	#endif
   592	      if (!application->force && most_likely_existing_cnf_file (arg))
   593	        ERROR ("not writing proof to '%s' file (use '-f')", arg);
   594	      if (!kissat_file_writable (arg))
   595	        ERROR ("can not write proof to '%s'", arg);
   596	      application->proof_path = arg;
   597	#else
   598	      ERROR ("two file arguments '%s' and '%s' without proof support "
   599	             "(try '-h')",
   600	             application->input_path, arg);
   601	#endif
   602	    } else {
   603	      if (!kissat_file_readable (arg))
   604	        ERROR ("can not read '%s'", arg);
   605	      application->input_path = arg;
   606	    }
   607	  }
   608	#ifndef KISSAT_HAS_COMPRESSION
   609	  if (!application->force && application->input_path &&
   610	      kissat_looks_like_a_compressed_file (application->input_path))
   611	    ERROR ("reading apparently compressed '%s' not supported "
   612	           "(use '-f' to force reading without decompression)",
   613	           application->input_path);
   614	#endif
   615	#if !defined(QUIET) && !defined(NOPTIONS)
   616	  if (kissat_get_option (solver, "quiet")) {
   617	    if (kissat_get_option (solver, "statistics"))
   618	      ERROR ("can not use '--quiet' ('-q') with '--statistics' ('-s')");
   619	    if (kissat_get_option (solver, "verbose"))
   620	      ERROR ("can not use '--quiet' ('-q') with '--verbose' ('-v')");
   662	  return true;
   663	}
   664	
   665	#ifndef NPROOFS
   666	
   667	static bool write_proof (application *application) {
   668	  const char *path = application->proof_path;
   669	  if (!path)
   670	    return true;
   671	  file *file = &application->proof_file;
   672	  bool binary = true;
   673	  if (!strcmp (path, "-")) {
   674	    binary = false;
   675	    kissat_write_already_open_file (file, stdout, "<stdout>");
   676	  } else if (!kissat_open_to_write_file (file, path))
   677	    ERROR ("failed to open and write proof to '%s'", path);
   678	  else if (application->binary < 0)
   679	    binary = false;
   680	  kissat_init_proof (application->solver, file, binary);
   681	#ifndef QUIET
   682	  kissat *solver = application->solver;
   683	  kissat_section (solver, "proving");
   684	  kissat_message (solver, "%swriting proof to %sDRAT file:",
   685	                  file->close ? "opened and " : "",
   686	                  file->compressed ? "compressed " : "");
   687	  kissat_line (solver);
   688	  kissat_message (solver, "  %s", file->path);
   689	#endif
   690	  return true;
   691	}
   692	
   693	static void close_proof (application *application) {
   694	  const char *path = application->proof_path;
   695	  if (!path)
   760	    if (application->decisions >= 0)
   761	      kissat_message (solver, "decision limit set to %d decisions",
   762	                      application->decisions);
   763	    else if (verbosity > 0)
   764	      kissat_message (solver, "no decision limit");
   765	  }
   766	}
   767	
   768	#endif
   769	
   770	static int run_application (kissat *solver, int argc, char **argv,
   771	                            bool *cancel_alarm_ptr) {
   772	  *cancel_alarm_ptr = false;
   773	  if (argc == 2)
   774	    if (parsed_one_option_and_return_zero_exit_code (argv[1]))
   775	      return 0;
   776	  application application;
   777	  init_app (&application, solver);
   778	  bool ok = parse_options (&application, argc, argv);
   779	  if (application.time > 0)
   780	    *cancel_alarm_ptr = true;
   781	  if (!ok)
   782	    return 1;
   783	#ifndef QUIET
   784	  kissat_section (solver, "banner");
   785	  if (!GET_OPTION (quiet)) {
   786	    kissat_banner ("c ", SOLVER_NAME);
   787	    fflush (stdout);
   788	  }
   789	#endif
   790	#ifndef NPROOFS
   791	  if (!write_proof (&application))
   792	    return 1;
   793	#endif
   794	  if (!parse_input (&application)) {
   795	#ifndef NPROOFS
   796	    close_proof (&application);
   797	#endif
   798	    return 1;
   799	  }
   800	#ifndef QUIET
   801	#ifndef NOPTIONS
   802	  print_options (solver);
   803	#endif
   804	  print_limits (&application);
   805	  kissat_section (solver, "solving");
   806	#endif
   807	  int res = kissat_solve (solver);
   808	#ifndef NPROOFS
   809	  close_proof (&application);
   810	#endif
   811	  kissat_section (solver, "result");
   812	  if (application.output_path && !strcmp (application.output_path, "-")) {
   813	#ifndef QUIET
   814	    const char *status;
   815	    if (res == 20)
   816	      status = "UNSATISFIABLE";
   817	    else if (res == 10)
   818	      status = "SATISFIABLE";
   819	    else
   820	      status = "UNKNOWN";
   821	    kissat_message (solver,
   822	                    "not printing 's %s' status line "
   823	                    "when writing DIMACS to '<stdout>'",
   824	                    status);
   825	#endif
   826	  } else {
   827	    if (res == 20) {
   828	      printf ("s UNSATISFIABLE\n");
   829	      fflush (stdout);
   830	    } else if (res == 10) {
   831	#ifndef NDEBUG
   832	      if (GET_OPTION (check))
   833	        kissat_check_satisfying_assignment (solver);
   834	#endif
   835	      printf ("s SATISFIABLE\n");
   836	      fflush (stdout);
   837	      if (application.witness)
   838	        kissat_print_witness (solver, application.max_var,
   839	                              application.partial);
   840	    } else {
   841	      printf ("s UNKNOWN\n");
   842	      fflush (stdout);
   843	    }
   844	  }
   845	  if (application.output_path) {
   846	    // TODO want to use 'struct file' from 'file.h'?
   847	    const char *path = application.output_path;
   848	    bool close_file;
   849	    FILE *file;
   850	    if (!strcmp (path, "-")) {
   851	      close_file = false;
   852	      file = stdout;
   853	    } else {
   854	      close_file = true;
   855	      file = fopen (path, "w");
   856	      if (!file)
   857	        ERROR ("could not write DIMACS file '%s'", path);
   858	    }
   859	    kissat_write_dimacs (solver, file);
   860	    if (close_file)
   861	      fclose (file);
   862	  }
   863	#ifndef QUIET
   864	  kissat_print_statistics (solver);
   865	#endif
   866	#ifndef QUIET
   867	  kissat_section (solver, "shutting down");
   868	  kissat_message (solver, "exit %d", res);
   869	#endif
   870	  return res;
   871	}
   872	
   873	int kissat_application (kissat *solver, int argc, char **argv) {
   874	  bool cancel_alarm;
   875	  int res = run_application (solver, argc, argv, &cancel_alarm);
   876	  if (cancel_alarm)
   877	    alarm (0);
   878	  return res;
   879	}
#include "error.h"
#include "colors.h"
#include "cover.h"

#include <stdarg.h>
#include <stdlib.h>

static void (*kissat_abort_function) (void);

void kissat_call_function_instead_of_abort (void (*f) (void)) {
  kissat_abort_function = f;
}

// clang-format off

void
kissat_abort (void)
{
  if (kissat_abort_function)
    { FLUSH_COVERAGE (); kissat_abort_function (); } // Keep all in this line.
  else
    { FLUSH_COVERAGE (); abort (); }                 // Keep all in this line.
}

// clang-format on

static void typed_error_message_start (const char *type) {
  fflush (stdout);
  TERMINAL (stderr, 2);
  COLOR (BOLD);
  fputs ("kissat: ", stderr);
  COLOR (RED);
  fputs (type, stderr);
  fputs (": ", stderr);
  COLOR (NORMAL);
}

void kissat_fatal_message_start (void) {
  typed_error_message_start ("fatal error");
}

static void vprint_error (const char *type, const char *fmt, va_list *ap) {
  typed_error_message_start (type);
  vfprintf (stderr, fmt, *ap);
  fputc ('\n', stderr);
  fflush (stderr);
}

void kissat_error (const char *fmt, ...) {
  va_list ap;
  va_start (ap, fmt);
  vprint_error ("error", fmt, &ap);
  va_end (ap);
}

void kissat_fatal (const char *fmt, ...) {
  va_list ap;
  va_start (ap, fmt);
  vprint_error ("fatal error", fmt, &ap);
  va_end (ap);
  kissat_abort ();
}

   616	int redundancyCheck (struct solver *S, int *clause, int size, int mark) {
   617	  int i, indegree;
   618	  int falsePivot = S->falseA[clause[PIVOT]];
   619	  if (S->verb) { printf ("\rc checking lemma (%i, %i) ", size, clause[PIVOT]); printClause (clause); }
   620

   622	    if ((clause[ID] & ACTIVE) == 0) return SUCCESS; }  // redundant?
   623	//    clause[PIVOT] ^= ACTIVE; }
   624	
   625	  if (size < 0) {
   626	    S->DB[ S->reason[abs (*clause)] - 2] |= 1;
   627	    return SUCCESS; }
   628	
   629	  indegree = S->nResolve;
   630	
   631	  S->RATmode = 0;
   632	  S->nDependencies = 0;

   634	    if (S->falseA[-clause[i]]) { // should only occur in forward mode
   635	      if (S->warning != NOWARNING) {
   636	        printf ("\rc WARNING: found a tautological clause in proof: "); printClause (clause); }
   637	      if (S->warning == HARDWARNING) exit (HARDWARNING);
   638	      while (S->forced < S->assigned) {
   639	        S->falseA[*(--S->assigned)] = 0;
   640	        S->reason[abs (*S->assigned)] = 0; }
   641	      return SUCCESS; }
   642	    S->falseA[clause[i]] = ASSUMED;
   643	    *(S->assigned++) = clause[i];
   644	    S->reason[abs (clause[i])] = 0; }
   645	
   646	  S->current = clause;
   647	  if (propagate (S, 0, mark) == UNSAT) {
   648	    indegree = S->nResolve - indegree;
   649	    if (indegree <= 2 && S->prep == 0) {
   650	      S->prep = 1; if (S->verb) printf ("\rc [%li] preprocessing checking mode on\n", S->time); }
   651	    if (indegree  > 2 && S->prep == 1) {
   652	      S->prep = 0; if (S->verb) printf ("\rc [%li] preprocessing checking mode off\n", S->time); }
   653	    if (S->verb) printf ("\rc lemma has RUP\n");
   654	    printDependencies (S, clause, 0);
   655	    return SUCCESS; }
   656	
   657	  // Failed RUP check.  Now test RAT.

   659	  if (S->rupOnly) {

   675	    failed = 1;

   677	      printf ("\rc WARNING: RAT check on proof pivot failed : "); printClause (clause); }
   678	    if (S->warning == HARDWARNING) exit (HARDWARNING);

   682	        clause[PIVOT] = clause[i];
   683	        failed = 0; break; } } }

   685	  if (failed == 0)
   686	    printDependencies (S, clause, 1);
   687	
   688	  S->processed = S->forced = savedForced;

   691	    S->reason[abs (*S->assigned)] = 0; }

   702	int init (struct solver *S) {
   703	  S->forced     = S->falseStack; // Points inside *falseStack at first decision (unforced literal)
   704	  S->processed  = S->falseStack; // Points inside *falseStack at first unprocessed literal
   705	  S->assigned   = S->falseStack; // Points inside *falseStack at last unprocessed literal
   706	
   707	  // initialize watch pointers on the original clauses
   708	  S->RATmode    = 0;
   709	  S->nRemoved   = 0;
   710	  S->nOpt       = 0;
   711	  S->nResolve   = 0;
   712	  S->RATcount   = 0;
   713	  S->nActive    = 0;
   714	  S->COREcount  = 0;
   715	  S->unitSize   = 0;
   716	
   717	  int i;
   718	  for (i = 1; i <= S->maxVar; ++i) {
   719	    S->reason    [i]                   = 0;
   720	    S->falseStack[i]                   = 0;
   721	    S->falseA[i]    = S->falseA[-i]    = 0;
   722	    S->used  [i]    = S->used  [-i]    = 0;
   723	    S->wlist [i][0] = S->wlist [-i][0] = END; }
   724	
   725	  for (i = 0; i < S->nClauses; i++) {
   726	    int *clause = S->DB + (S->formula[i] >> INFOBITS);
   727	    if (clause[ID] & ACTIVE) clause[ID] ^= ACTIVE;
   728	    if (clause[0] == 0) {
   729	      printf ("\rc formula contains empty clause\n");
   730	      if (S->coreStr) {
   731	        FILE *coreFile = fopen (S->coreStr, "w");
   732	        fprintf (coreFile, "p cnf 0 1\n 0\n");
   733	        fclose (coreFile); }
   734	      if (S->lemmaStr) {
   735	        FILE *lemmaFile = fopen (S->lemmaStr, "w");
   736	        fprintf (lemmaFile, "0\n");
   737	        fclose (lemmaFile); }
   738	      return UNSAT; }
   739	    if (clause[1]) { addWatch (S, clause, 0); addWatch (S, clause, 1); }
   740	    else if (S->falseA[clause[0]]) {
   741	      printf ("\rc found complementary unit clauses: %i\n", clause[0]);
   742	      if (S->coreStr) {
   743	        FILE *coreFile = fopen (S->coreStr, "w");
   744	        fprintf (coreFile, "p cnf %i 2\n%i 0\n%i 0\n", abs (clause[0]), clause[0], -clause[0]);
   745	        fclose (coreFile); }
   746	      if (S->lemmaStr) {
   747	        FILE *lemmaFile = fopen (S->lemmaStr, "w");
   748	        fprintf (lemmaFile, "0\n");
   749	        fclose (lemmaFile); }
   750	      if (S->lratFile) {
   751	        int j;
   752	        for (j = 0; j < i; j++) {
   753	          int *_clause = S->DB + (S->formula[j] >> INFOBITS);
   754	          if ((_clause[0] == -clause[0]) && !_clause[1]) break; }
   755	        fprintf (S->lratFile, "%li 0 %i %i 0\n", S->nClauses + 1, j + 1, i + 1); }
   756	      return UNSAT; }
   757	    else if (!S->falseA[ -clause[0] ]) {
   758	      addUnit (S, (long) (clause - S->DB));
   759	      assign (S, clause[0]); } }
   760	
   761	  S->nDependencies = 0;
   762	  S->time = S->count; // Alternative time init
   763	  if (propagateUnits (S, 1) == UNSAT) {
   764	    printf ("\rc UNSAT via unit propagation on the input instance\n");
   765	    printDependencies (S, NULL, 0);
   766	    postprocess (S); return UNSAT; }
   767	  return SAT; }
   768	
   769	int verify (struct solver *S, int begin, int end) {
   770	  int top_flag = 1;
   771	  if (init (S) == UNSAT) return UNSAT;
   772	
   773	  if (S->mode == FORWARD_UNSAT) {
   774	    if (begin == end)
   775	      printf ("\rc start forward verification\n"); }
   776	
   777	  int step;
   778	  int adds = 0;
   779	  int active = S->nClauses;
   780	  for (step = 0; step < S->nStep; step++) {
   781	    if (step >= begin && step < end) continue;
   782	    long ad = S->proof[step]; long d = ad & 1;
   783	    int *lemmas = S->DB + (ad >> INFOBITS);
   784	
   785	    S->time = lemmas[ID];
   786	    if (d) { active--; }
   787	    else   { active++; adds++; }
   788	    if (S->mode == FORWARD_SAT && S->verb) printf ("\rc %i active clauses\n", active);
   789	
   790	    if (!lemmas[1]) { // found a unit
   791	      int lit = lemmas[0];
   792	      if (S->verb)
   793	        printf ("\rc found unit in proof %i [%li]\n", lit, S->time);
   794	      if (d) {
   795	        if (S->mode == FORWARD_SAT) {
   796	          removeUnit (S, lit); propagateUnits (S, 0); }
   797	        else { // no need to remove units while checking UNSAT
   798	          if (S->verb) { printf("c removing proof step: d "); printClause(lemmas); }
   799	          S->proof[step] = 0; continue; } }
   800	      else {
   801	//        printf ("c unit %i\n", lit);
   802	//        if (S->falseA[-lit]) S->reason[abs(lit)] = (lemmas - S->DB) + 1;
   803	        if (S->mode == BACKWARD_UNSAT && S->falseA[-lit]) { S->proof[step] = 0; continue; }
   804	        else { addUnit (S, (long) (lemmas - S->DB)); } } }
   805	
   806	    if (d && lemmas[1]) { // if delete and not unit
   807	      if ((S->reason[abs (lemmas[0])] - 1) == (lemmas - S->DB)) { // what is this check?
   808	        if (S->mode != FORWARD_SAT) { // ignore pseudo unit clause deletion
   809	          if (S->warning != NOWARNING) {

+    def reap_robust(self):
+        """r3#1: exception-resistant reap state machine; retries on any exception, never unwinds
+        before wait() returns.  Single SIGTERM (only if requested), quarantine at the 60 s transition."""
+        while True:

+                    if self.proc.poll() is None:

+def completed_checker(rec, lines, expect):
+    """r3#8: a completed drat-trim outcome: exactly 's VERIFIED' rc0 or 's NOT VERIFIED' rc1, no
+    MEMOUT/TIMEOUT/ERROR text, healthy settlement."""

     9	#include <sys/types.h>
    10	#include <unistd.h>
    11	
    12	bool kissat_file_exists (const char *path) {
    13	  if (!path)
    14	    return false;
    15	  struct stat buf;
    16	  if (stat (path, &buf))
    17	    return false;
    18	  return true;
    19	}
    20	
    21	bool kissat_file_readable (const char *path) {
    22	  if (!path)
    23	    return false;
    24	  struct stat buf;
    25	  if (stat (path, &buf))
    26	    return false;
    27	  if (access (path, R_OK))
    28	    return false;
    29	  return true;
    30	}
    31	
    32	bool kissat_file_writable (const char *path) {
    33	  int res;
    34	  if (!path)
    35	    res = 1;
    36	  else if (!strcmp (path, "/dev/null"))
    37	    res = 0;
    38	  else {
    39	    if (!*path)
    40	      res = 2;
    41	    else {
    42	      struct stat buf;
    43	      const char *p = strrchr (path, '/');
    44	      if (!p) {
    45	        if (stat (path, &buf)) {
    46	          if (errno == ENOENT)
    47	            res = 0;
    48	          else
    49	            res = -2;
    50	        } else if (S_ISDIR (buf.st_mode))
    51	          res = 3;
    52	        else if (access (path, W_OK))
    53	          res = 4;
    54	        else
    55	          res = 0;
    56	      } else if (!p[1])
    57	        res = 5;
    58	      else {
    59	        const size_t len = p - path;
    60	        char *dirname = malloc (len + 1);
    61	        if (dirname) {
    62	          strncpy (dirname, path, len);
    63	          dirname[len] = 0;
    64	          if (stat (dirname, &buf))
    65	            res = 6;
    66	          else if (!S_ISDIR (buf.st_mode))
    67	            res = 7;
    68	          else if (access (dirname, W_OK))
    69	            res = 8;
    70	          else if (stat (path, &buf)) {
    71	            if (errno == ENOENT)
    72	              res = 0;
    73	            else
    74	              res = -3;
    75	          } else if (access (path, W_OK))
    76	            res = 9;
    77	          else
    78	            res = 0;
    79	          free (dirname);
    80	        } else
    81	          res = 10;
    82	      }
    83	    }
    84	  }
    85	  return !res;
    86	}
    87	
    88	size_t kissat_file_size (const char *path) {
    89	  struct stat buf;
    90	  if (stat (path, &buf))
    91	    return 0;
    92	  return (size_t) buf.st_size;
    93	}
    94	
    95	bool kissat_find_executable (const char *name) {
    96	  const size_t name_len = strlen (name);
    97	  const char *environment = getenv ("PATH");
    98	  if (!environment)
    99	    return false;
   100	  const size_t dirs_len = strlen (environment);
   101	  char *dirs = malloc (dirs_len + 1);
   102	  if (!dirs)
   103	    return false;
   104	  strcpy (dirs, environment);
   105	  bool res = false;
   106	  const char *end = dirs + dirs_len + 1;
   107	  for (char *dir = dirs, *q; !res && dir != end; dir = q) {
   108	    for (q = dir; *q && *q != ':'; q++)
   109	      assert (q + 1 < end);
   110	    *q++ = 0;
   111	    const size_t path_len = (q - dir) + name_len;
   112	    char *path = malloc (path_len + 1);
   113	    if (!path) {
   114	      free (dirs);
   115	      return false;
   116	    }
   117	    sprintf (path, "%s/%s", dir, name);
   118	    assert (strlen (path) == path_len);
   119	    res = kissat_file_readable (path);
   120	    free (path);
   121	  }
   122	  free (dirs);
   123	  return res;
   124	}

     1	#include "allocate.h"
     2	#include "error.h"
     3	#include "internal.h"
     4	#include "logging.h"
     5	
     6	#undef LOGPREFIX
     7	#define LOGPREFIX "ALLOCATE"
     8	
     9	#include <string.h>
    10	
    11	#ifdef LOGGING
    12	#include <inttypes.h>
    13	#endif
    14	
    15	static void inc_bytes (kissat *solver, size_t bytes) {
    16	#ifdef METRICS
    17	  if (!solver)
    18	    return;
    19	  ADD (allocated_current, bytes);
    20	  LOG5 ("allocated_current = %s",
    21	        FORMAT_BYTES (solver->statistics.allocated_current));
    22	  if (solver->statistics.allocated_current >=
    23	      solver->statistics.allocated_max) {
    24	    solver->statistics.allocated_max = solver->statistics.allocated_current;
    25	    LOG5 ("allocated_max = %s",
    26	          FORMAT_BYTES (solver->statistics.allocated_max));
    27	  }
    28	#else
    29	  (void) solver;
    30	  (void) bytes;
    31	#endif
    32	}
    33	
    34	static void dec_bytes (kissat *solver, size_t bytes) {
    35	#ifdef METRICS
    36	  if (!solver)
    37	    return;
    38	  SUB (allocated_current, bytes);
    39	  LOG5 ("allocated_current = %s",
    40	        FORMAT_BYTES (solver->statistics.allocated_current));
    41	#else
    42	  (void) solver;
    43	  (void) bytes;
    44	#endif
    45	}
    46	
    47	void *kissat_malloc (kissat *solver, size_t bytes) {
    48	  void *res;
    49	  if (!bytes)
    50	    return 0;
    51	  res = malloc (bytes);
    52	  LOG4 ("malloc (%zu) = %p", bytes, res);
    53	  if (!res)
    54	    kissat_fatal ("out-of-memory allocating %zu bytes", bytes);
    55	  inc_bytes (solver, bytes);
    56	  return res;
    57	}
    58	
    59	void kissat_free (kissat *solver, void *ptr, size_t bytes) {
    60	  if (ptr) {
    61	    LOG4 ("free (%p[%zu])", ptr, bytes);
    62	    dec_bytes (solver, bytes);
    63	    free (ptr);
    64	  } else
    65	    assert (!bytes);
    66	}
    67	
    68	char *kissat_strdup (kissat *solver, const char *str) {
    69	  char *res = kissat_malloc (solver, strlen (str) + 1);
    70	  return strcpy (res, str);
    71	}
    72	
    73	void kissat_freestr (struct kissat *solver, char *str) {
    74	  assert (str);
    75	  kissat_free (solver, str, strlen (str) + 1);
    76	}
    77	
    78	void *kissat_nalloc (kissat *solver, size_t n, size_t size) {
    79	  void *res;
    80	  if (!n || !size)
    81	    return 0;
    82	  if (MAX_SIZE_T / size < n)
    83	    kissat_fatal ("invalid 'kissat_nalloc (..., %zu, %zu)' call", n, size);
    84	  const size_t bytes = n * size;
    85	  res = malloc (bytes);
    86	  LOG4 ("nalloc (%zu, %zu) = %p", n, size, res);
    87	  if (!res)
    88	    kissat_fatal ("out-of-memory allocating "
    89	                  "%zu = %zu x %zu bytes",
    90	                  bytes, n, size);
    91	  inc_bytes (solver, bytes);
    92	  return res;
    93	}
    94	
    95	void *kissat_calloc (kissat *solver, size_t n, size_t size) {
    96	  void *res;
    97	  if (!n || !size)
    98	    return 0;
    99	  if (MAX_SIZE_T / size < n)
   100	    kissat_fatal ("invalid 'kissat_calloc (..., %zu, %zu)' call", n, size);
   101	  res = calloc (n, size);
   102	  LOG4 ("calloc (%zu, %zu) = %p", n, size, res);
   103	  const size_t bytes = n * size;
   104	  if (!res)
   105	    kissat_fatal ("out-of-memory allocating "
   106	                  "%zu = %zu x %zu bytes",
   107	                  bytes, n, size);
   108	  inc_bytes (solver, bytes);
   109	  return res;
   110	}
   111	
   112	void kissat_dealloc (kissat *solver, void *ptr, size_t n, size_t size) {
   113	  if (!n || !size)
   114	    return;
   115	  if (MAX_SIZE_T / size < n)
   116	    kissat_fatal ("invalid 'kissat_dealloc (..., %zu, %zu)' call", n, size);
   117	  const size_t bytes = n * size;
   118	  kissat_free (solver, ptr, bytes);
   119	}
   120	
   121	void *kissat_realloc (kissat *solver, void *p, size_t old_bytes,
   122	                      size_t new_bytes) {
   123	  if (old_bytes == new_bytes)
   124	    return p;
   125	  if (!new_bytes) {
   126	    kissat_free (solver, p, old_bytes);
   127	    return 0;
   128	  }
   129	  dec_bytes (solver, old_bytes);
   130	#ifdef LOGGING
   131	  if (GET_OPTION (log) > 3)
   132	    kissat_begin_logging (solver, LOGPREFIX, "realloc (%p[%zu, %zu) = ", p,
   133	                          old_bytes, new_bytes);
   134	#endif
   135	  void *res = realloc (p, new_bytes);
   136	#ifdef LOGGING
   137	  if (GET_OPTION (log) > 3) {
   138	    printf ("%p", res);
   139	    kissat_end_logging ();
   140	  }
   141	#endif
   142	  if (new_bytes && !res)
   143	    kissat_fatal ("out-of-memory reallocating from %zu to %zu bytes",
   144	                  old_bytes, new_bytes);
   145	  inc_bytes (solver, new_bytes);
   146	  return res;
   147	}
   148	
   149	void *kissat_nrealloc (kissat *solver, void *p, size_t o, size_t n,
   150	                       size_t size) {
   151	  if (!size) {
   152	    assert (!p);
   153	    assert (!o);
   154	    return 0;
   155	  }
   156	  const size_t max = MAX_SIZE_T / size;
   157	  if (max < o || max < n)
   158	    kissat_fatal ("invalid 'kissat_nrealloc (..., %zu, %zu, %zu)' call", o,
   159	                  n, size);
   160	  return kissat_realloc (solver, p, o * size, n * size);
   161	}
     1	#include "handle.h"
     2	
     3	#include <assert.h>
     4	#include <stdbool.h>
     5	#include <stdio.h>
     6	
     7	static void (*handler) (int);
     8	static volatile int caught_signal;
     9	static volatile bool handler_set;
    10	
    11	// clang-format off
    12	
    13	#define SIGNAL(SIG) \
    14	static void (*SIG ## _handler)(int);
    15	SIGNALS
    16	#undef SIGNAL
    17	
    18	void
    19	kissat_reset_signal_handler (void)
    20	{
    21	  if (!handler_set)
    22	    return;
    23	#define SIGNAL(SIG) \
    24	  signal (SIG, SIG ## _handler);
    25	  SIGNALS
    26	#undef SIGNAL
    27	  handler_set = false;
    28	  handler = 0;
    29	}
    30	
    31	// clang-format on
    32	
    33	static void catch_signal (int sig) {
    34	  if (caught_signal)
    35	    return;
    36	  caught_signal = sig;
    37	  assert (handler_set);
    38	  assert (handler);
    39	  handler (sig);
    40	  kissat_reset_signal_handler ();
    41	  raise (sig);
    42	}
    43	
    44	void kissat_init_signal_handler (void (*h) (int sig)) {
    45	  assert (!handler);
    46	  handler = h;
    47	  handler_set = true;
    48	#define SIGNAL(SIG) SIG##_handler = signal (SIG, catch_signal);
    49	  SIGNALS
    50	#undef SIGNAL
    51	}
    52	
    53	static volatile bool caught_alarm;
    54	static volatile bool alarm_handler_set;
    55	static void (*volatile SIGALRM_handler) (int);
    56	static void (*volatile handle_alarm) (void);
    57	
    58	static void catch_alarm (int sig) {
    59	  assert (sig == SIGALRM);
    60	  if (caught_alarm)
    61	    return;
    62	  caught_alarm = true;
    63	  static void (*volatile handler) (void);
    64	  handler = handle_alarm;
    65	  if (!alarm_handler_set)
    66	    raise (sig);
    67	  assert (handler);
    68	  handler ();
    69	}
    70	
    71	void kissat_init_alarm (void (*handler) (void)) {
    72	  assert (handler);
    73	  assert (!caught_alarm);
    74	  handle_alarm = handler;
    75	  alarm_handler_set = true;
    76	  assert (!SIGALRM_handler);
    77	  SIGALRM_handler = signal (SIGALRM, catch_alarm);
    78	}
    79	
    80	void kissat_reset_alarm (void) {
    81	  assert (alarm_handler_set);
    82	  assert (handle_alarm);
    83	  alarm_handler_set = false;
    84	  handle_alarm = 0;
    85	  (void) signal (SIGALRM, SIGALRM_handler);
    86	}

    54	void kissat_init_proof (kissat *solver, file *file, bool binary) {
    55	  assert (file);
    56	  assert (!solver->proof);
    57	  proof *proof = kissat_calloc (solver, 1, sizeof (struct proof));
    58	  proof->binary = binary;
    59	  proof->file = file;
    60	  proof->solver = solver;
    61	  solver->proof = proof;
    62	  LOG ("starting to trace %s proof", binary ? "binary" : "non-binary");
    63	}
    64	
    65	static void flush_buffer (proof *proof) {
    66	  size_t bytes = proof->buffer.pos;
    67	  if (!bytes)
    68	    return;
    69	  size_t written = kissat_write (proof->file, proof->buffer.chars, bytes);
    70	  if (bytes != written)
    71	    kissat_fatal ("flushing %zu bytes in proof write-buffer failed", bytes);
    72	  proof->buffer.pos = 0;
    73	}
    74	
    75	void kissat_release_proof (kissat *solver) {
    76	  proof *proof = solver->proof;
    77	  assert (proof);
    78	  LOG ("stopping to trace proof");
    79	  flush_buffer (proof);
    80	  kissat_flush (proof->file);
    81	  RELEASE_STACK (proof->line);
    82	#ifndef NDEBUG
    83	  kissat_free (solver, proof->units, proof->size_units);
    84	#endif
    85	#if !defined(NDEBUG) || defined(LOGGING)
    86	  RELEASE_STACK (proof->imported);
    87	#endif
    88	  kissat_free (solver, proof, sizeof (struct proof));
    89	  solver->proof = 0;

   188	static void print_binary_proof_line (proof *proof) {
   189	  assert (proof->binary);
   190	  for (all_stack (int, elit, proof->line)) {
   191	    unsigned x = 2u * ABS (elit) + (elit < 0);
   192	    unsigned char ch;
   193	    while (x & ~0x7f) {
   194	      ch = (x & 0x7f) | 0x80;
   195	      write_char (proof, ch);
   196	      x >>= 7;
   197	    }
   198	    write_char (proof, x);
   199	  }
   200	  write_char (proof, 0);
   201	}
   202	
   203	static void print_non_binary_proof_line (proof *proof) {
   204	  assert (!proof->binary);
   205	  char buffer[16];
   206	  char *end_of_buffer = buffer + sizeof buffer;
   207	  *--end_of_buffer = 0;
   208	  for (all_stack (int, elit, proof->line)) {
   209	    char *p = end_of_buffer;
   210	    assert (!*p);
   211	    assert (elit);
   212	    assert (elit != INT_MIN);
   213	    unsigned eidx;
   214	    if (elit < 0) {
   215	      write_char (proof, '-');
   216	      eidx = -elit;
   217	    } else
   218	      eidx = elit;
   219	    for (unsigned tmp = eidx; tmp; tmp /= 10)
   220	      *--p = '0' + (tmp % 10);
   221	    while (p != end_of_buffer)
   222	      write_char (proof, *p++);
   223	    write_char (proof, ' ');
   224	  }
   225	  write_char (proof, '0');
   226	  write_char (proof, '\n');
   227	}
   228	
   229	static void print_proof_line (proof *proof) {
   230	  proof->lines++;
   231	  if (proof->binary)
   232	    print_binary_proof_line (proof);
   233	  else
   234	    print_non_binary_proof_line (proof);
   235	  CLEAR_STACK (proof->line);
   236	#if !defined(NDEBUG) || defined(LOGGING)
   237	  CLEAR_STACK (proof->imported);
   238	#endif
   239	#ifndef NOPTIONS
   240	  kissat *solver = proof->solver;
   241	#endif
   242	  if (GET_OPTION (flushproof)) {
   243	    flush_buffer (proof);
   244	    kissat_flush (proof->file);
   245	  }
   246	}

   250	static unsigned external_to_proof_literal (int elit) {
   251	  assert (elit);
   252	  assert (elit != INT_MIN);
   253	  return 2u * (abs (elit) - 1) + (elit < 0);
   254	}

   256	static void resize_proof_units (proof *proof, unsigned plit) {
   257	  kissat *solver = proof->solver;
   258	  const size_t old_size = proof->size_units;
   259	  size_t new_size = old_size ? old_size : 2;
   260	  while (new_size <= plit)
   261	    new_size *= 2;
   262	  char *new_units = kissat_calloc (solver, new_size, 1);
   263	  if (old_size)
   264	    memcpy (new_units, proof->units, old_size);
   265	  kissat_dealloc (solver, proof->units, old_size, 1);
   266	  proof->units = new_units;
   267	  proof->size_units = new_size;
   268	}
   269	
   270	static void check_repeated_proof_lines (proof *proof) {
   271	  size_t size = SIZE_STACK (proof->line);
   272	  if (!size) {
   273	    assert (!proof->empty);
   274	    proof->empty = true;
   275	  } else if (size == 1) {
   276	    const int eunit = PEEK_STACK (proof->line, 0);
   277	    const unsigned punit = external_to_proof_literal (eunit);
   278	    assert (punit != INVALID_LIT);
   279	    if (!proof->size_units || proof->size_units <= punit)
   280	      resize_proof_units (proof, punit);
   281	    proof->units[punit] = 1;
   282	  }
   283	}

   706	static void print_option (kissat *solver, int value, const opt *o) {
   707	  char buffer[96];
   708	  const bool b = (o->low == 0 && o->high == 1);
   709	  const char *val_str = FORMAT_VALUE (b, value);
   710	  const char *def_str = FORMAT_VALUE (b, o->value);

   683	
   684	def run_child(argv, label, wall, reserve, inputs, sink=None):
   685	    """Prepared outputs -> admission -> spawn -> supervise -> settle.  Returns (rec|None, lines, refusal)."""
   686	    ch = None
   687	    try:
   688	        ch = Child(argv, label, wall, reserve, inputs, sink)
   689	        if ch.refused:
   690	            return None, [], ch.refused
   691	        peak = None
   692	        try:
   693	            if CANCEL.is_set():
   694	                ch.term("owner_cancel_sigterm")
   695	            _, peak = supervise(ch, sink)
   696	        except BaseException as exc:
   697	            latch("runner exception during supervision %r" % exc)
   698	            ch.term("runner_exception")
   699	        rec = ch.settle()
   700	        rec["trip"] = ch.stop
   701	        rec["peak_sampled_lane_rss_bytes"] = peak
   702	        lines = segments(ch.out_path) if rec.get("stream_thread_alive_after_join") is False else []
   703	        return rec, lines, []
   704	    finally:
   705	        if ch is not None and getattr(ch, "proc", None) is not None and ch.settled_rec is None:
   706	            ch.settle()  # any escape above still reaps and settles the owned child
   707	        if sink is not None and (ch is None or getattr(ch, "proc", None) is None):
   708	            sink.finish()  # never-spawned producer: unblock and join the reader
   709	
   710	
   711	def run_kissat(cnf_path, proof_file, label, seconds, cap, reserve, inputs):
   712	    argv = [KISSAT[0], "-q", "-f", "--seed=1", "--time=%d" % seconds, cnf_path]
   713	    sink = None
   714	    if proof_file is not None:
   715	        fifo_dir = os.path.join(STATE["out"], "fifo")
   716	        os.makedirs(fifo_dir, exist_ok=True)
